- **#130** ★ [L] _medium_ **Hoist constant key-value pairs out of JSON records** — In NDJSON logs (pino, zap, slog, docker --json) and record arrays, find keys with identical values on nearly every record (service, host, pid, type, version). Print once in a header, delete per row, keep exceptions inline.
  - Test: On sample NDJSON and GitHub list outputs, measure the share of key-value pairs constant on 90% or more of rows, and token delta after hoisting.
- **#131** ★ [R] _medium_ `MCP` **Auto-mine derivable JSON fields instead of hand blocklists** — Per MCP tool, detect fields whose value is a template of sibling fields (html_url from repo plus number, *_url from url, node_id) or constant across a corpus. Emit drop lists automatically; originals stay in the saved file.
  - Test: Run the miner over captured GitHub, Jira and Slack MCP outputs; list candidate fields with removable tokens; hand-review 30 for false positives.
- **#132** ★ [R] _large_ `MCP` **Sample long JSON arrays and cap depth, keeping outliers** — For arrays of 100+ uniform objects keep first and last k rows, error or failed rows, z-score numeric outliers, plus schema and total count; collapse subtrees below depth d to key lists. Targets docker inspect, kubectl -o json, cargo metadata, list-type MCP results.
  - Test: On 20 captured outputs, ask 5 questions each; compare accuracy against columnar-only encoding at equal tokens and count questions the sampled view cannot answer.
- **#133** ★ [R] _medium_ **Elide long numeric arrays: embeddings, coordinates, tensors** — Replace homogeneous number arrays over about 32 items with '[1536 floats, min..max, first 4: ...]'. Targets embedding API results, GeoJSON coordinates, histogram buckets, numpy and torch prints. Different from rounding floats.
  - Test: Regex-scan transcripts for runs of 64 or more numbers; sum their token share; check follow-up commands never needed the elided values.
- **#135** ★ [R] _medium_ **OpenAPI spec digest into an endpoint table** — Collapse openapi.json or swagger.yaml fetched via curl or doc MCPs to 'METHOD /path (params) -> status:schema' lines. Resolve $ref once in a components table; drop examples, x- extensions, repeated 4xx boilerplate, descriptions duplicating summary.
  - Test: Digest Petstore and a large public spec; compare chars and tokens; ask 10 endpoint and parameter questions against digest versus original.
- **#136** [R] _small_ `MCP` **Unwrap GraphQL edges/node; drop __typename and cursors** — In gh api graphql and GraphQL MCP results, turn edges[].node into a plain array and drop __typename, per-edge cursor, and pageInfo when hasNextPage is false. The flat array then feeds columnar encoding. Keep the cursor when more pages exist.
  - Test: Run a first:50 issues query via gh api graphql; compare tokens before and after; confirm pagination still works from the retained endCursor.
- **#145** ★ [L] _medium_ `MCP` **MCP JSON entity cache across calls and pages** — Hash nested objects (user, repo, label, milestone) per MCP server within a session. Repeats in later pages or list-then-get calls become a short ref to the earlier occurrence. Within-output dedup misses this.
  - Test: Replay GitHub MCP list_issues, list_pull_requests and issue_read outputs from a session; count bytes of nested objects identical to ones in earlier responses.
- **#228** [L] _small_ **Join byte-separated hex runs: hexdump, xxd, openssl** — Space or colon separated hex bytes tokenize far worse than joined runs. o200k proxy: 64 spaced bytes 151 tokens vs 72 joined; an openssl-style colon block 610 vs 348. Optionally also drop the hexdump -C ASCII gutter.
  - Test: Run xxd, hexdump -C, od -x, openssl x509 -text and ssh-keygen -lf; join runs; tokenize before and after; assert splitting back into pairs reproduces the original bytes.
- **#230** [R] _small_ **Decode percent-escapes, HTML entities and quoted-printable** — Escapes cost 2-3 tokens per byte. o200k proxy: UTF-8 percent run 36 vs 16 tokens, apostrophe and quote entities 34 vs 16, quoted-printable 48 vs 9. Decode only UTF-8 and unreserved chars; keep %2F %26 %3D %23 encoded. Targets access logs, curl'd HTML/RSS, raw .eml.
  - Test: Scan transcripts for %XX, &#NN; and =XX runs; decode with the guards; tokenize before and after; assert re-encoding each decoded span reproduces the original.
- **#233** [R] _small_ **Stub binary-looking output by size, type and hash** — cat of a binary, curl without -o, or git show of an image yields NUL, C0 control and U+FFFD runs. 300 random bytes decoded with replacement cost 221 tokens vs 12 for a stub with size, magic-byte type and sha. Also drop private-use icon glyphs.
  - Test: Set a control/FFFD density threshold; run cat on ELF, PNG, zip and SQLite files; verify the stub fires; confirm text files with tabs or UTF-8 never trigger.
- **#254** [R] _small_ `MCP` **Collapse mutating MCP results to a short acknowledgement** — create_, update_, add_, push_ and merge_ tools return the object the model just wrote (reportedly full PR or comment JSON), often under the 6,000-char threshold. Keep id, url, state, error; save the rest.
  - Test: Collect results of write-verb MCP calls from transcripts, size them in tokens, and check which fields the next five turns referenced; simulate the ack form and count misses.
- **#258** [R] _small_ `MCP` **Truncate *_at ISO timestamps in MCP JSON lists** — created_at, updated_at, closed_at and pushed_at repeat on every item. On an o200k proxy, 2025-10-30T12:34:56Z is 13 tokens, date-only 6. Truncate to date or minute for keys matching *_at; keep the original in the saved file.
  - Test: Count *_at fields and tokens in captured GitHub, Jira and Linear list outputs; apply truncation; check whether agents ever need seconds or timezone (sort order, staleness questions).
- **#262** [M] _small_ `MCP` **Learn MCP key-path drop lists from classifier scores** — Aggregate the classifier's mean keep-score per JSON key path across a server's responses; paths scoring low in over 90% of responses join a per-tool drop list. Catches verbose boilerplate fields that are not derivable from siblings, unlike derivable-field mining.
  - Test: Run on 100 captured GitHub or Jira MCP responses; list top 30 paths by chars removed; hand-review false positives; check JSON still parses and QA holds.
- **#264** [R] _small_ `MCP` **Markdown section skeleton for READMEs and docs pages** — Parse headings; keep the outline, each section's first sentence and fenced code blocks, and elide other bodies as '[Install: 1.2k chars elided]'. Targets cat README.md, docs fetched via MCP, changelogs. Structure-aware alternative to atom deletion on prose.
  - Test: 30 README, docs and changelog outputs over 6k chars; ask where-is and how-to questions; compare accuracy and tokens with the classifier at 0.5.
- **#316** [L] _small_ **Rewrite [{Key,Value}] tag and env lists as maps** — AWS Tags [{Key,Value}], Kubernetes env [{name,value}] and similar pair lists pay braces and key names per entry; columnar encoding suits big uniform arrays, not 3-entry tag lists. Synthetic: pretty 65, minified 31, map 17 tokens.
  - Test: Convert Tags, Labels and env lists in describe-instances and kubectl -o json samples; assert reverse conversion round-trips including duplicate keys; tokenize; test follow-up --filter commands.
- **#322** ★ [R] _medium_ **Render .ipynb as percent-format cells, cap outputs, stub images** — cat, jq, git show or Read of .ipynb prints kernelspec, execution_count, per-line source arrays and base64 outputs. Render '# %%' cells with indices, cap text outputs, stub image/png. Tiny notebook: 276 vs 26 tokens (o200k proxy). Needs an .ipynb exception to the cat skip.
  - Test: Convert 10 real notebooks with nbformat; tokenize before and after; check images dominate; ask cell and output questions on both forms; confirm NotebookEdit by cell index still hits.
- **#323** ★ [L] _medium_ **Unwrap syntax-highlighter span soup in HTML code blocks** — Pygments, highlight.js, Prism and Shiki wrap every token in a classed span; dropping attributes (#39) leaves the tags. Two-line Python sample: 101 tokens vs 11 plain (o200k proxy). Flatten pre/code to text, drop line-number cells. Targets curl'd docs pages and fetch MCPs.
  - Test: curl 10 docs pages with code; flatten pre blocks via lxml text_content; assert text equals the entity-decoded original; compare tokens with full HTML-to-markdown.
- **#324** ★ [R] _medium_ **Rich-text AST JSON to Markdown: ADF, Notion, Slack blocks** — Jira REST v3 returns text as Atlassian Document Format; Notion rich_text and Slack blocks nest similarly, and Slack blocks duplicate text. Synthetic ADF: 308 tokens indented, 198 minified, 28 as Markdown (o200k proxy). Convert known node types, pass unknown ones through.
  - Test: Capture a Jira v3 issue, a Notion page and Slack history; convert; compare tokens; diff extracted plain text between original and converted; list node types dropped.
- **#325** ★ [L] _medium_ **Decode base64 text content fields in API JSON** — GitHub contents API (gh api, curl) returns file content as 60-column base64 with encoding: base64. Eliding blobs (#41) would lose the file. 3 KB of Python: 2573 tokens as base64 vs 783 decoded (o200k proxy). Decode only valid UTF-8 without NULs; skip secrets.
  - Test: Run gh api contents on 5 files; decode when encoding is base64; check re-encoding round-trips; tokenize both; confirm Kubernetes Secret data is left alone by default.
- **#327** [R] _small_ **Known license texts and headers to SPDX stubs** — Match LICENSE, NOTICE, vendored licenses.txt and per-file headers against normalised SPDX license-list text (licensee and ScanCode do similar matching, reportedly); emit '[Apache-2.0, 11 KB]'. Apache header: 129 vs 20 tokens (o200k proxy). Exact matches only.
  - Test: Scan 20 repos plus node_modules for license text; count tokens in grep -r 'Licensed under' hits and cat LICENSE; run matcher; verify modified licenses are never stubbed.
- **#331** [R] _small_ **Stub SVG path data and editor metadata** — Replace d="..." path strings with a length stub; drop inkscape/sodipodi namespaces, namedview and default style attributes; keep ids, text, viewBox and structure. Synthetic Inkscape icon: 282 vs 37 tokens (o200k proxy). Targets grep -r hits and reads of icon sets.
  - Test: Collect 20 SVGs from icon packs and Inkscape exports; measure token share of d= and editor metadata; ask which text labels and ids exist, before and after.
- **#333** [R] _small_ **Markdown pipe tables re-serialised as TSV with header note** — Beyond trimming padding (#38): a 21-row, 4-column table cost 444 tokens padded, 398 minimal pipe, 325 TSV, 346 CSV (o200k proxy). Convert only when cells hold no tabs, pipes or newlines; HTML tables too. Reader accuracy per format needs checking.
  - Test: Take 30 README and report tables; convert; ask 10 lookup and aggregate questions per table on Haiku, Sonnet and Opus; compare accuracy and tokens, pipe versus TSV.
- **#407** ★ [L] _medium_ **Hoist constant and default-index columns from text tables** — Hoist columns with one distinct value into a header line, and drop a default 0..N-1 index column with a note, in pandas print, to_csv, psql, mlflow search_runs. Synthetic 50-row CSV, 3 of 18 columns constant: 6744 to 6389 tokens (o200k).
  - Test: Run head/to_csv on 20 real datasets; count tokens in constant and RangeIndex columns; ask row-lookup questions on original versus hoisted form.
- **#408** [L] _small_ **Strip column-uniform trailing zeros from printed floats** — pandas pads every cell in a column to equal decimals (describe() prints 200.000000). Dropping trailing zeros keeps values identical, unlike rounding (#44). Synthetic 6-decimal columns: 279 vs 189 tokens (o200k). Also sklearn reports and numpy prints.
  - Test: Tokenise describe(), classification_report and DataFrame prints before and after zero-stripping; assert float() parse equality for every cell.
- **#412** ★ [L] _medium_ **ASCII +---+ grid tables to TSV (Spark show, mysql)** — Spark show(), mysql, beeline and sqlite print +-----+ border rows and a | on every line; psql adds a header rule. Convert to TSV when cells hold no tabs. 2-row synthetic grid: 55 vs 18 tokens (o200k). Not box glyphs (#15) or markdown (#38).
  - Test: Capture show(), mysql and psql output with 50 rows; convert; assert cell-for-cell equality after parsing the grid; tokenise both; ask lookup questions.
- **#420** [L] _small_ **Regular-interval time series: encode as start, step, values** — Prometheus and Loki query_range matrices store [timestamp, value] pairs at fixed steps. When steps are regular, emit start, step and a flat value list; irregular gaps stay explicit. Synthetic 30-point series: 333 vs 148 tokens (o200k proxy).
  - Test: Query a local Prometheus with query_range; re-encode; assert expansion round-trips exactly; tokenise; check the agent still finds the peak value and its time.
- **#421** ★ [R] _medium_ **OTLP, Jaeger and Zipkin trace JSON to indented span tree** — Trace JSON carries typed attribute wrappers ({key,value:{stringValue}}), 32-hex trace ids and 19-digit nanosecond epochs per span. Render a parent-child tree with name, duration, start offset from root, status, unwrapped attributes; trace id once. Wrapper alone: 13 vs 5 tokens (o200k proxy).
  - Test: Export a trace from a local OTel collector or Jaeger all-in-one; render the tree; tokenise both forms; ask which span is slowest and which errored, on both.
- **#424** ★ [R] _medium_ **journalctl -o json: keep a field allowlist** — Each journal JSON entry carries about 20 per-boot or per-unit fields (__CURSOR, _BOOT_ID, _MACHINE_ID, _SYSTEMD_CGROUP, _CAP_EFFECTIVE, _SELINUX_CONTEXT). Keep timestamp, unit, PID, PRIORITY, MESSAGE; hoist constants. Synthetic entry: 351 vs 42 tokens (o200k proxy).
  - Test: Run journalctl -o json -n 200 on a systemd host; rank key frequencies; drop the complement of the allowlist; tokenise; confirm error messages and unit names survive.
- **#429** [R] _small_ **Error-event frame objects to compact traceback lines** — Sentry-style events and Jackson-serialized StackTraceElement arrays hold each frame as an object (filename, abs_path, module, lineno, in_app, pre_context, post_context, vars). Render 'file:line in func', keep in_app frames, vars for the top frame only. Synthetic frame: 68 vs 7 tokens (o200k proxy).
  - Test: Log a Java exception as JSON with a logstash encoder and build a Sentry-shaped event; convert; tokenise; check the agent locates the failing line from the compact form.
- **#480** [R] _small_ `MCP` **Fold resolved and outdated review threads; one diff_hunk per thread** — Review-comment JSON repeats the same diff_hunk on every reply in a thread. Use isResolved and isOutdated (GraphQL reviewThreads, reportedly also in MCP review output) to reduce closed threads to path:line, first comment and a count; print each hunk once for open ones.
  - Test: Pull review comments for 10 PRs with long threads; measure token share of resolved threads and duplicate hunks; ask which feedback remains unaddressed, folded versus raw.
- **#481** [R] _small_ **Fold long details blocks in issue and PR markdown** — Reporters wrap env dumps, pip freeze and full logs in <details><summary>; the author already marked them as secondary. Keep summary plus first and last 5 lines when the block exceeds 40 lines, with a line count; the original stays in the saved file.
  - Test: Sample 50 bug issues containing details; measure their token share; fold; ask for root cause and versions, and count cases where the answer lived in the folded middle.
- **#519** ★ [L] _medium_ **Minify multi-document or truncated JSON with a lexeme-preserving scanner** — jq '.[]' output, concatenated docs and truncated JSON fail json.loads, so parse-based minify skips them. A string-aware scanner strips whitespace outside strings and keeps number text; loads/dumps turns 1E5 into 100000.0 and merges duplicate keys. Ten pretty docs: 680 vs 340 tokens (o200k proxy).
  - Test: Run jq '.[]' output and head -c truncated API JSON through it; assert whitespace-stripped input equals output byte for byte; fuzz with 1E5, duplicate keys, 20-digit ints.
- **#520** ★ [L] _medium_ **Partition mixed-kind JSON arrays by key set, columnar per group** — #32 assumes uniform rows, but timelines, CloudTrail, k8s events and Slack history mix shapes. Group rows by key-set signature or discriminator (event, type, kind); emit one columnar table per group of 3+, leave singletons inline. Synthetic 36 events: 1130 vs 670 tokens (o200k proxy).
  - Test: Capture gh api issues/N/timeline, aws cloudtrail lookup-events and kubectl get events -o json; encode; assert row-for-row reconstruction; ask per-kind counting questions against minified JSON.
- **#521** ★ [R] _medium_ **Render GraphQL introspection, JSON Schema and CRDs as type signatures** — Introspection JSON nests kind/name/ofType wrapper chains (one field: 71 tokens vs 6 as 'issues: [Issue!]!'); CRD schemas repeat type/description blocks (synthetic spec: YAML 108, signature 45). Emit SDL or 'name?: type // desc' lines, drop __ types, resolve $ref once. #248 only strips boilerplate.
  - Test: Fetch introspection JSON from a public GraphQL API; run kubectl explain --recursive and get crd for cert-manager; convert; tokenise; ask required fields and enum values; list dropped keywords.
- **#522** ★ [L] _medium_ **Rewrite NDJSON app logs as logfmt before line rules** — pino, zap, slog and bunyan lines carry quotes, braces and repeated keys that hide them from timestamp, template and duplicate-line rules. Emit 'time LEVEL msg k=v' with dotted nested keys, quoting only when needed. Synthetic 20 lines: 1220 vs 759 tokens (o200k proxy).
  - Test: Convert pino and zap samples; assert key=value parse returns the original objects; run timestamp, template and duplicate rules on both forms; compare tokens against #130 hoisting alone.
- **#523** [R] _small_ **Drop rendered-HTML and epoch twins of fields in records** — The same value stored twice in one record: Jira renderedFields HTML beside wiki text, GitHub body_html beside body, RSS content:encoded beside description, epoch beside ISO time. Detect by equality after tag-strip or instant parse; keep the plainer form. Synthetic: 82 vs 31 tokens (o200k proxy).
  - Test: Capture Jira issues with expand=renderedFields and a GitHub issue with the full+json media type; measure twin token share; check stripped-HTML similarity finds twins without dropping genuinely different text.
- **#524** [R] _small_ **Collapse all-zero counter objects such as GitHub reactions** — Lone zeros can matter (#33), but an object whose numeric members are all 0 (reactions: +1, -1, laugh, hooray, heart, rocket, eyes, plus url) carries no information. Fold it to {}; lone zero fields stay. One reactions object: 59 vs 5 tokens (o200k proxy).
  - Test: Count all-zero reactions objects in captured GitHub list_issues and list_pull_requests output; collapse them; tokenise; confirm non-zero reaction objects stay verbatim.
- **#525** [L] _small_ **Strip indentation whitespace from pretty-printed XML: pom, csproj, manifests** — Generic counterpart of JSON minify (#31) for XML read by cat or curl: drop whitespace-only text nodes between elements, never under xml:space=preserve or in mixed content. Synthetic pom dependencies: 696 vs 485 tokens (o200k proxy). #40 covers only reports.
  - Test: Take 20 pom.xml, AndroidManifest.xml, .csproj and SOAP files; strip with lxml remove_blank_text; assert trees equal ignoring blank text; tokenise before and after. Exclude files the agent will Edit.
- **#526** [L] _tiny_ **Remove unneeded quotes from quote-all CSV output** — Exports using QUOTE_ALL, pandas quoting=1 or spreadsheet dumps wrap every field in quotes. Re-emit with minimal quoting (only fields with comma, quote or newline); keep quotes on numeric-looking text. Synthetic 30 rows: 368 vs 336 tokens (o200k proxy).
  - Test: Collect quote-all CSVs from pandas quoting=1 and spreadsheet exports; rewrite with csv.QUOTE_MINIMAL; assert csv.reader rows equal before and after; tokenise.
- **#527** [R] _tiny_ **Collapse textproto map entries and hoist grpcurl @type URLs** — Protobuf text prints each map entry as a key/value message; grpcurl repeats the type.googleapis.com prefix per Any. Fold entries into 'labels: {a: b}' when a repeated field has only key and value; hoist the prefix. 6 entries: 90 vs 28 tokens (o200k).
  - Test: Capture grpcurl responses and a .textproto/.pbtxt file; apply the folds; tokenise; assert expansion restores the entries; ask which map keys exist on both forms.
- **#528** [L] _small_ **Flow-style YAML for scalar-only block sequences** — Lists such as container args, ports and paths cost a newline, indent and dash per item. Rewrite plain-scalar-only sequences as '[a, b, c]' when no item has a colon-space, hash, comma or bracket and no comments interleave. 12 args: 110 vs 87 tokens (o200k proxy).
  - Test: Apply to kubectl get -o yaml and docker-compose output; assert yaml.safe_load is equal before and after; tokenise; exclude Read and cat of repo files, since Edit needs exact lines.
- **#534** [L] _small_ **RFC 6902-style delta for re-fetched same-id JSON entities** — Polling an issue, PR, pod or job returns the whole object with two fields changed. Key by server, type and id; emit changed paths plus 'N fields unchanged'. #145 only references identical objects; #62 is line-based.
  - Test: Fetch one GitHub issue via gh api before and after a comment; tokenise the full response versus the path delta. Repeat with kubectl get pod -o json across a rollout.
- **#596** [R] _small_ **Knee-point cutoff to pick how many rows to keep** — Headroom's SmartCrusher reportedly uses the Kneedle elbow algorithm to find where extra array items stop adding information. Score rows by novelty (deflate cost, or distance to nearest kept row), sort, and keep rows up to the knee instead of fixed first/last k (#132).
  - Test: Take 30 recorded JSON lists, grep and find outputs over 200 rows; compare Kneedle keep counts against fixed 0.5 for rows kept and answers to count and outlier questions.
- **#606** ★ [R] _large_ **npm packument and PyPI JSON API to version digest** — Measured: the express packument is 823 KB (310k o200k-proxy tokens) and PyPI's requests JSON 193 KB (82k). A digest of dist-tags, latest deps and engines, plus one 'version date' line per release, was 4.2k and 2.7k tokens. Targets curl and npm view --json.
  - Test: Curl registry JSON for 10 packages; project to the digest; ask latest version, date of a 4.x release and engines.node; compare answers and tokens.
- **#612** [R] _small_ **Decode JWTs to claims JSON plus signature stub** — JWTs (eyJ... three base64url parts) in curl -v, env dumps and OAuth responses tokenise poorly. Synthetic JWT: 144 vs 63 o200k-proxy tokens when decoded with the signature stubbed. Keep exp, sub, iss, aud; original saved.
  - Test: Regex eyJ[\w-]+\.eyJ[\w-]+\.[\w-]+ over transcripts; decode header and payload; tokenise before and after; check expiry and audience questions remain answerable.
- **#617** [R] _small_ **Strip thousands separators from consistently grouped numeric columns** — Grouped integers like 1,234,567 tokenise as digit and comma pieces. Synthetic 30 large integers: 179 vs 119 tokens (o200k proxy). Apply only when every number in a column or run matches the grouping pattern; keep decimals; add a header note.
  - Test: Scan saved outputs for \d{1,3}(,\d{3})+ runs; strip in consistent columns; assert reversal restores the original; tokenise; ask sum and max questions before and after.
- **#619** [L] _tiny_ **Decode Python bytes-repr and \xNN UTF-8 escape runs** — print(resp.content), CalledProcessError and socket reprs show b'\xe6\x97\xa5...' at about 3 tokens per byte. Synthetic CJK plus accented string: 58 vs 7 tokens (o200k proxy). Decode only runs forming valid UTF-8; keep \n and \x00.
  - Test: Regex b'...' literals and \x runs in saved outputs; decode valid UTF-8; assert re-encoding round-trips; tokenise before and after.
- **#639** [L] _small_ `MCP` **Cross-call key factoring for repeated singleton MCP objects** — Each issue_read, get_commit or get_label result repeats the same key names. After the first result per tool in a session, render later singleton objects as values only under a 'same keys as call N' header. Differs from within-output columnar encoding (#32) and entity caching (#145).
  - Test: Replay recorded MCP singleton results per tool; compute key-name tokens in calls 2+; run reader QA on the values-only form, including after a simulated compaction dropping call 1.
- **#640** [R] _small_ `MCP` **Drop response fields that echo the call's own arguments** — Some MCP results repeat the request: query, owner/repo, page, perPage, or a repository object per item for the repo the model just named. Compare tool_input with tool_response and delete values equal to arguments, noting the removal once.
  - Test: For recorded MCP calls, tokenise response values that exactly equal a tool_input value; list the top keys; check the next turns never cited them.
- **#641** [R] _tiny_ `MCP` **Collapse single-key wrapper objects in MCP JSON results** — Some servers wrap payloads as {"data":{"result":[...]}}, {"response":...} or {"success":true,"data":...}. Peel wrappers whose only informative child is one value, with a path note, saving braces, keys and indentation per call. Differs from GraphQL edges/node unwrapping (#136).
  - Test: Survey recorded MCP results for top-level objects with one non-status key; count peelable depth and tokens; confirm the note lets a reader still locate the payload.
- **#652** [M] _small_ **Indentation-tree folding scored by block maximum atom score** — Parse any indented output (describe, plan, YAML, Debug and pprint dumps) into a block tree; fold subtrees deeper than d whose max classifier atom score is low into 'name: (14 lines)'. Generic non-JSON counterpart of depth capping (#132) and per-tool packs.
  - Test: On 25 kubectl describe, terraform, pytest -vv and Debug dumps, compare tokens, needle recall and QA against flat atom deletion at equal size.
- **#704** [R] **Unwrap xcresulttool typed-value JSON: _type/_value scalars** — Legacy 'xcrun xcresulttool get --format json' reportedly wraps every scalar as {"_type":{"_name":"String"},"_value":"x"} and arrays as _values (from memory, unmeasured). Rewrite to plain values, drop _type for primitives, keep it for objects. Newer Xcode subcommands may emit leaner JSON.
  - Test: Verify that the installed Xcode still emits the wrappers: capture xcresulttool output from a 50-test run; unwrap; compare tokens; ask which tests failed and their durations on both forms.
- **#705** [R] _small_ **Markdown links: collapse [X](X), drop relative targets, hoist URLs** — Bot comments render [https://...](https://...), the same string twice; collapsing that is lossless. Then drop relative and #anchor targets, move external URLs to numbered definitions at the end, optionally strip ** markers. Measured: link targets were 13% of o200k tokens over 107 markdown files.
  - Test: Run on 30 READMEs, changelogs, PR bodies and docs pages; tokenise before and after; ask 'where is X documented' and 'which PR' questions; saved original keeps every URL.
- **#706** [R] _small_ **Drop contents lists that restate later headings** — doctoc blocks, [TOC] lists, 'On this page' sidebars, anchor-only README lists and PDF contents pages restate headings that follow. When entries match later headings, replace the list with a one-line note. Measured: TOC lines were 5,360 of 710,687 o200k tokens in 300 READMEs.
  - Test: Take 20 READMEs, docs pages and pdftotext outputs with contents lists; drop with a title-match check; ask where-is-section questions on both versions; compare tokens.
- **#707** [R] _small_ **Replace image embeds and badge rows with alt-text stubs** — READMEs, docs pages and issue markdown carry shields.io badge rows, screenshots, linked logos and img tags the model cannot see. Replace with [img: alt] or a count. Measured: badge lines held 19,043 of 710,687 o200k tokens in 300 READMEs. Extends #326 (issue bodies only).
  - Test: Count image and badge tokens in 30 READMEs and docs pages; replace; confirm alt text survives; ask which CI and license badges exist; keep the original in the saved file.
- **#708** [R] _small_ **Digest the HTML head: keep title, description, canonical** — Raw curl output opens with a head full of hreflang alternates, preload and icon links, and og:/twitter: meta. Keep title, description, canonical, robots and lang; fold the rest to counts. Synthetic 15 hreflang links: 406 o200k tokens.
  - Test: curl 15 sites; measure head token share; keep listed fields; ask 'what is the canonical URL and page language' and a link-hunting question; check no body content is dropped.
- **#709** [R] _small_ **Strip docs-site chrome left after HTML-to-Markdown conversion** — Converted docs pages keep per-heading permalink links ('Direct link to', pilcrow, #), 'Skip to main content', 'Edit this page', 'Was this page helpful?', pagers and cookie text. Drop by anchored regex and phrase list. Synthetic 10-heading page: 249 vs 70 o200k tokens.
  - Test: Convert 15 Docusaurus, Sphinx and MkDocs pages with html2text or pandoc; count chrome tokens; strip; assert prose and code blocks unchanged; compare with #39.
- **#710** [R] _small_ **Keep only the project's language in multi-SDK doc examples** — API reference pages show one example per SDK language (curl, Python, JS, Go, Java); HTML-to-Markdown inlines all variants. Detect adjacent fences with different info strings, keep the languages in the repo's manifests, and stub the rest as '[Go, Java variants omitted]'.
  - Test: Fetch 10 API reference pages; count runs of 3+ adjacent fences with differing languages and their token share; ask 'show the call in our language' questions.
- **#713** [R] _small_ **Replace XML closing-tag names with </> in display-only reads** — Indented XML (pom, Android layouts, OOXML, SOAP) repeats each element name in its closing tag. Emit '</>' and rely on nesting and indentation. Synthetic: OOXML 266 vs 194 tokens, pom 336 vs 280 (o200k). Conflicts with #525 indent stripping; choose one per file.
  - Test: Apply to 20 XML files; tokenise; ask 'which element contains X' and 'what is the parent of Y' on both; mark failures when single-line or unindented; exclude files to edit.
- **#714** [R] _small_ **Sitemap XML to prefix-hoisted URL lists** — sitemap.xml and sitemap indexes fetched by curl wrap each URL in loc, lastmod, changefreq and priority tags. Print a base prefix plus one path per line, optionally with lastmod only; drop changefreq and priority. Synthetic 30-URL sitemap: 2,200 vs 191 o200k tokens.
  - Test: Fetch 10 real sitemaps; convert; assert the loc set is identical after re-expansion; tokenise; ask 'is /x/y listed' and 'newest page' questions.
- **#732** ★ [R] _medium_ **Strip defaults and float noise from design-file JSON** — Figma REST node JSON repeats blendMode, visible, opacity 1, empty effects/strokes and 17-digit 0-1 float colours and coordinates. Drop defaults, convert colours to hex, round to 2 decimals. Synthetic node: 129 vs 38 tokens (o200k). Lottie, Sketch and Excalidraw are likely similar.
  - Test: Fetch a real Figma file via the nodes endpoint; tokenise before and after; assert names, hierarchy and text survive; ask 10 layout and colour questions.
- **#761** [R] _small_ **Transpose vector DB parallel-array results into rows** — Chroma-style query results return nested parallel lists (ids, documents, metadatas, distances as [[...]]) plus null embeddings, uris and data keys. Emit one row per hit, drop nulls, round distances to 3 decimals. Synthetic 8-hit result: 450 vs 280 o200k tokens.
  - Test: Run chromadb locally, query 8 hits, capture the result dict, apply the rows transform, verify id-to-document-to-metadata alignment by round trip and compare tokens.
- **#766** [L] _small_ **Header-once table for CSV-loader row documents** — LangChain CSVLoader and LlamaIndex row readers emit 'column: value' lines per row, repeating every column name in each retrieved document. Group same-schema documents; print the header once, then tab-separated values. Synthetic 10 rows of 8 columns: 539 vs 277 o200k tokens. Cf. #410.
  - Test: Load an 8-column CSV with CSVLoader, retrieve top-10 rows, convert to header plus rows, check every cell round-trips to its column, compare tokens.
- **#778** [L] _tiny_ **Ditto marks for run-repeated cells in text tables** — kubectl get pods -A, ps, docker ps and git log tables repeat namespace, status or author on consecutive rows; replace repeats with a quote mark after a one-line legend. Synthetic 48-row pod table: 1,097 to 923 o200k tokens; #407 only hoists fully constant columns.
  - Test: Apply to 20 captured kubectl, ps and git log tables; count real-tokenizer tokens; ask cell-lookup questions (#418) of a reader on ditto versus plain form.
- **#792** [L] _small_ **YAML anchors for repeated label blocks within one document** — kubectl get List output repeats identical labels and annotations mappings per item. Emit the mapping once as an anchor and alias it; still valid YAML. Anchors are scoped per document, so they cannot cross --- boundaries. Synthetic 5-label block: 60 tokens, alias 6.
  - Test: Render a 12-item kubectl get List; hoist mappings seen 3+ times; assert yaml.safe_load of both forms is equal; tokenize; ask which items carry a given label.
- **#796** [R] _tiny_ **Stub boto3 ResponseMetadata and tz-aware datetime reprs** — Scripts that print(client.call()) show ResponseMetadata (RequestId twice, HTTPHeaders, RetryAttempts) and datetime.datetime(..., tzinfo=tzutc()) reprs. Stub metadata when HTTPStatusCode is 200; rewrite reprs to ISO. Synthetic: metadata 142 tokens, datetime repr 31 versus ISO 13.
  - Test: Run moto-backed boto3 scripts printing list and describe responses; apply stub and ISO rewrite; assert the same instants; tokenize before and after.
- **#801** ★ [R] _medium_ **Elasticsearch and OpenSearch hit envelopes to flat rows** — Search responses wrap each hit in _index, _id, _score, sort and a 'fields' map of single-element arrays that duplicate _source. Hoist _index, drop took/_shards/_score, unwrap singleton arrays, print one line per hit. Synthetic 5 hits: 677 vs 74 o200k.
  - Test: Save one Kibana Dev Tools or curl _search response; run the rewrite and check every _source value and the total survive.
- **#807** [R] _small_ **Percentile tables and histograms to eight quantile rows** — HdrHistogram .hgrm files, wrk/hey/vegeta latency histograms, ab percentile tables and Prometheus _bucket series list dozens of cumulative rows. Keep p50, p90, p99, p99.9, max, total count and the mode bucket; save the rest. Not measured; target is rows with long-run repeated counts.
  - Test: Generate a .hgrm from a 100k-sample load test, apply the cut, and compare tokens; check quantiles match the original rows.
- **#862** [R] _small_ **Drop commit verification payload and signature blocks** — GitHub commit JSON carries verification.signature and verification.payload, reportedly the full PGP or SSH signature plus the signed commit text again; git log --show-signature and cat-file print the same blocks. Synthetic PGP block: 410 o200k tokens. Keep verified and reason.
  - Test: Fetch list_commits for a repo with signed commits; measure verification field share; stub; ask who authored and whether verified, folded versus raw.
- **#879** [L] _small_ **Rejoin backslash-wrapped pandas DataFrame column chunks** — With display.max_columns=None, pandas splits wide frames into backslash-continued column chunks, repeating the index in each. Rejoin chunks into one tab-separated table with the index once. Synthetic 30x14 frame: 2417 vs 2032 o200k tokens.
  - Test: Print 10 wide frames; rejoin chunks, assert cell equality against df.to_csv, tokenise both forms, ask cell-lookup questions on each.
- **#880** ★ [R] _medium_ **Decode JSON-escaped source lines in notebook git diffs** — git diff of .ipynb shows each code line as a quoted JSON string ending in \n, plus execution_count and metadata hunks on every re-run. Decode to plain lines, fold counter and output hunks. Synthetic hunk: 98 vs 34 o200k tokens. Reading notebooks is #322.
  - Test: Re-run a real notebook and git diff it; render a per-cell plain diff; confirm code edits survive and output hunks collapse to counts; tokenise before and after.
- **#881** [L] _small_ **Print symmetric matrices as triangles, sparse ones as cells** — df.corr(), covariance, distance and similarity matrices repeat values across the diagonal; confusion matrices are mostly zeros. If exactly symmetric, print the upper triangle with a note; if mostly zero, list nonzero cells. Synthetic 12x12 corr: 498 tokens, 389 for triangle numbers alone (ceiling).
  - Test: Print corr() for 10 datasets and a 50-class confusion matrix; check exact symmetry, expand back to the identical matrix, ask 'most correlated pair' on both forms.
- **#882** [L] _small_ **Fold psql \x and mysql \G records into one table** — Expanded mode prints '-[ RECORD n ]-' or '*** n. row ***' then a padded name|value line per field, repeating column names every row. Parse runs of records sharing a column list into one header plus value rows. #410 covers only one-line records.
  - Test: Run psql \x and mysql \G on a 20-row, 15-column table; convert to a table, assert cell equality, tokenise both, ask which row has the max of a column.
- **#884** [R] _small_ **Schemas from Spark, BigQuery, Glue, Avro as name-type lines** — PySpark StructType reprs, printSchema '(nullable = true)' rails, bq show prettyjson field objects, Glue get-table Columns and .avsc files spend a nested object or call per column. Emit 'name type' lines, flagging only non-default nullability or mode. Synthetic 5-column repr: 55 vs 16 o200k tokens.
  - Test: Capture df.schema, printSchema, bq show --format=prettyjson and glue get-table for five wide tables; convert, round-trip name-type pairs, tokenise, ask one column's type.
- **#886** ★ [R] _medium_ **Digest dbt manifest, run_results and catalog JSON reads** — target/manifest.json carries each node's raw_code, compiled_code, macros, docs and checksums; run_results.json repeats timing arrays and adapter_response per node (from memory, unmeasured). On cat or jq reads emit per-node name, materialization, depends_on, status and failure message; full file stays on disk.
  - Test: Compile a sample dbt project, cat its manifest and run_results; rank key families by token share, build the digest, ask upstream models of X and failed tests on both.
- **#891** ★ [R] _medium_ **Reassemble SSE and streaming-chunk JSON into final text** — curl -N of OpenAI- or Anthropic-style streams yields one data: JSON envelope per token, repeating id, model, created. Concatenate delta text and tool-call argument fragments; keep first-chunk metadata, finish_reason, usage, errors. Synthetic 66 chunks: 3,752 vs 48 o200k tokens.
  - Test: Capture streamed completions with curl -N; reassemble; assert text equals the non-stream response; tokenise both; confirm mid-stream error events and truncated streams survive.
- **#892** [R] _small_ **Unwrap DynamoDB, Firestore and Mongo extended-JSON type tags** — aws dynamodb scan wraps values as {"S":..}, {"N":".."}, {"M":{..}}; Firestore uses stringValue/mapValue.fields; Mongo canonical EJSON uses $oid, $date, $numberLong. A known-wrapper table gives plain values plus a legend. Synthetic 20 items: 1,319 vs 762 (DynamoDB), 1,282 vs 1,002 (EJSON) o200k tokens.
  - Test: Run aws dynamodb scan and mongoexport --jsonFormat canonical on sample data; unwrap; assert reverse mapping restores input; tokenise; ask attribute-type questions on both.
- **#893** [R] _small_ **Digest CycloneDX, SPDX and syft SBOM JSON to component lines** — Each component repeats bom-ref and purl, derivable cpe, hashes and tool properties; dependency edges repeat those refs. Emit 'name@version license' lines plus edges by short index. Synthetic 40 components: 8,382 vs 369 o200k tokens (repetitive hashes, so indicative only).
  - Test: Run npm sbom --sbom-format cyclonedx or syft on a small repo; digest; tokenise by field class; ask licence and depends-on questions on both forms.
- **#895** [L] _small_ **Plist XML key/value pairs to compact typed key: value lines** — Info.plist, entitlements and launchd plists spell each pair as <key>K</key><string>V</string> under a DOCTYPE header. Render type-preserving key: value lines (bool, int, date markers), round-trip checked via plistlib. Synthetic 7-key Info.plist: 246 vs 74 o200k tokens. #525 only strips whitespace.
  - Test: Convert 20 real Info.plist, entitlements and LaunchAgents files; assert plistlib.loads equality after reverse conversion; tokenise before and after; skip files about to be edited.
- **#896** [L] _small_ **Fold Relay Connection and Edge boilerplate types in GraphQL SDL** — In schema.graphql, every object type has XConnection{edges,nodes,pageInfo,totalCount} and XEdge{cursor,node} twins. When they match the template exactly, print one legend line plus a name list; deviations stay verbatim. Synthetic 8 types: 378 vs 37 o200k tokens. #521 covers introspection JSON only.
  - Test: Fetch a public SDL (GitHub's schema.docs.graphql); count types matching the template exactly; fold; assert unfolding reproduces the file byte for byte; tokenise.
- **#897** [L] _small_ **Sparse wide CSV and TSV: emit only non-empty cells** — Feature matrices, exported forms and wide report CSVs are mostly empty cells: runs of commas. Past roughly 70% empty, print the header once, then rows as col=value for filled cells, keeping row order. Synthetic 40 columns, 3 filled: 609 vs 479 o200k tokens.
  - Test: Find wide CSVs in saved outputs; compute empty-cell share; convert rows; assert reverse conversion restores the CSV; tokenise; ask which rows have column X filled.
- **#898** ★ [L] _medium_ **Skeleton-plus-slots fold for near-identical sibling JSON subtrees** — #35 needs exact equality, #32 flat rows. OpenAPI paths (CORS OPTIONS, API Gateway mock integrations), CloudFormation resources and k8s items share one nested skeleton differing in few leaves. Print the skeleton once, then per-sibling leaf overrides. Synthetic 25 paths: 4,564 vs 761 o200k tokens.
  - Test: Export an API Gateway OpenAPI file and a CloudFormation template; cluster siblings by structural hash; fold; assert expansion restores parsed equality; measure the real foldable share.
- **#899** [R] _small_ **Strip type and length tags from var_dump and spew** — PHP var_dump prints string(5) "Alice" and array(3) {; Go spew prints (string) (len=5) and (main.User) per value; lengths are derivable. Rewrite to JSON-like, quotes preserving types, object ids dropped. Synthetic 24 rows: var_dump 1,238 and spew 992 vs 347 o200k tokens. #808 only joins lines.
  - Test: Capture var_dump of a Laravel or WordPress object and spew.Dump of a Go struct; convert; verify values and nesting match; tokenise; ask field-value questions on both.
- **#900** [L] _small_ **Lift escaped multi-line JSON strings into raw side blocks** — Stack traces, diffs and file bodies inside JSON strings keep \n and \" escapes, so line rules (traceback, dedupe, templates) cannot see them. Replace with a placeholder; print the raw string after the JSON. Synthetic 24-line trace: 405 vs 376 tokens; mainly enables other rules.
  - Test: Take pino or Sentry JSON with stack fields; lift strings; run existing line rules on both forms; assert unlifting restores parsed equality; compare tokens.
- **#985** [L] _small_ **Unescape over-escaped Markdown punctuation from HTML converters** — HTML-to-Markdown converters (reportedly turndown, pandoc) emit \_ \. \( \[ \! on plain words, so snake\_case names cost extra and defeat grep. Unescape where inert; skip line-start 1\., table pipes and code spans. Synthetic sentence: 47 vs 32 tokens (o200k proxy).
  - Test: Run fetch, Notion and Confluence MCP pages through the rule; assert markdown-it HTML render is identical before and after; tokenise both; count backslash-punctuation share.
- **#986** [R] _small_ **Unwrap Python scalar reprs: np.float64(), Decimal(), PosixPath()** — NumPy 2 scalar repr (NEP 51) prints np.float64(0.1) inside every printed list or dict; Decimal('1.5'), PosixPath('x'), UUID('..') and pandas Timestamp('..') do likewise. Unwrap single-line reprs to the inner literal. Synthetic list: 30 vs 18 tokens (o200k proxy).
  - Test: Print numpy 2 scalars, pytest assert diffs and pandas row dicts; unwrap by regex; assert inner literal text unchanged; tokenise; ask type questions to see whether the lost wrapper matters.
- **#987** [L] _small_ **Drop optional spaces in Python dict, list and call reprs** — print(d), pprint, pytest asserts and logging emit ', ' and ': ' in single-quoted reprs that JSON minify (#31) never matches. Scan outside string literals and delete the space; foo(a, b) argument lists too. Synthetic dict: 49 vs 37 tokens, args 18 vs 15 (o200k).
  - Test: Capture 50 repr lines from Python runs; delete separator spaces outside quotes; assert ast.literal_eval equality and string-literal bytes unchanged; tokenise; leave file-read output untouched.
- **#988** [L] _small_ **Canonicalise printf-style scientific notation like 1.234560e+02** — C printf, Fortran, numpy and solver logs print padded mantissas with signed two-digit exponents. Rewrite digit-exact with Decimal to 123.456 or -9.87e-5, never through float. Synthetic line: 31 vs 19 tokens (o200k proxy).
  - Test: Grep saved outputs for [0-9]e[+-][0-9]{2}; rewrite via Decimal; assert Decimal(old)==Decimal(new) for every match; skip hex-like tokens; tokenise before and after.
- **#992** [R] _tiny_ `MCP` **Strip bold and italic markers from MCP Markdown prose** — Issue, PR and Notion bodies and bot comments wrap words in ** and _ . Remove paired emphasis outside code fences and inline code; keep bullets, snake_case and globs. Synthetic emphasised sentence: 31 vs 17 tokens; real share unknown until #715 measures it.
  - Test: Apply to 100 saved issue and PR bodies; count markers per 1k tokens; assert no identifier or glob changed; QA warnings like 'DO NOT' for lost emphasis.
- **#1015** [M] _small_ `MCP` **Mine JSON key-path keep lists from fields agents later reference** — Across transcripts, record which MCP response key paths have values later copied into tool inputs or assistant text. Paths almost never referenced become drop candidates, referenced ones never-drop. Aggregates behaviour per schema path, unlike atom labels (#169) or classifier scores (#262).
  - Test: On 100 captured GitHub MCP list responses plus the following turns, compute per-path reference rate; compare chars removed with #262's list; hand-check false drops.
- **#1019** [M] _small_ **Parse-first prose islands: score only Markdown text nodes and comments** — Parse Markdown, docstrings and code comments; run the classifier only on paragraph, list-item and comment text, leaving fences, links, table rows and code byte-identical. Extends JSON-string-only scoring (#134); avoids post-hoc delimiter repair (#777).
  - Test: On 50 READMEs and issue bodies, count broken fences, links and tables versus whole-text pruning at 0.5; compare QA at equal tokens.
- **#1067** ★ [R] _medium_ **Strip SSR markers and stub hydration payloads in fetched HTML** — A curl'd Next.js page: flight scripts held 42.8k of 90k o200k tokens, inline SVG 15k. Stub __NEXT_DATA__, flight pushes and __APOLLO_STATE__ to key list plus size; strip React '<!-- -->', Angular _ngcontent-*/ng-reflect-*, Vue data-v-* markers. Extends #39.
  - Test: curl SSR pages from Next, Nuxt, Angular and SvelteKit apps; measure payload and marker token share; assert the visible-text DOM dump is unchanged; ask a reader to locate one prop.
- **#1075** [R] _small_ **Dictionary-stub known scaffold comments and XML prologs** — Hash lines against a shipped table of upstream text and drop or stub exact matches only: tsc --init, jest --init, Django settings.py and alembic.ini comments; pom schemaLocation roots, SVG and plist DOCTYPEs. Pom prolog 89 vs 14 tokens; user comments survive. Like #327.
  - Test: Generate each scaffold, scan 100 local XML files for known prologs; tokenise their share; drop exact matches only; confirm user comments survive and a reader still names the schema version.
- **#1077** [R] _small_ **Fold tool-owned marker blocks in Markdown READMEs** — All-contributors lists (ALL-CONTRIBUTORS-LIST:START/END), terraform-docs (BEGIN_TF_DOCS) and similar generated regions hold avatar img and href cells per person. Fold each marker pair to names plus a count. One synthetic cell: 128 vs 7 o200k tokens. Related to #707.
  - Test: Grep 30 popular READMEs for START/END and BEGIN/END marker pairs; measure token share inside them; confirm names survive the fold and the original stays in the saved file.
- **#1081** [L] _small_ **Lossless HTML attribute minifier: booleans, quotes, defaults** — Complements #39's chrome stripping: disabled="disabled" to disabled, unquote simple attributes, drop type="text/javascript", method="get", empty attributes and void-tag slashes. Same parse tree. Synthetic form fragment: 53 vs 31 o200k tokens.
  - Test: Parse 30 curl'd pages and templates with html5lib before and after; assert identical trees; tokenise; exclude edit targets; compare the gain with #39 alone.
- **#1086** [R] _small_ **Changelog digest: newest sections plus versions named in context** — cat of CHANGELOG.md, HISTORY, NEWS or a release body often runs years deep. Keep Unreleased and the latest N version sections plus any section whose version appears in the command, prompt or lockfile diff; fold the rest to a heading list with counts.
  - Test: Take 30 changelogs; split by version headings; run 20 'what changed in vX' and 'when was Y removed' questions with and without the digest; count refetches.
- **#1154** ★ [R] _medium_ **Xcode pbxproj and .sln: alias opaque IDs, collapse config matrices** — project.pbxproj repeats 24-hex object IDs beside name comments; .sln lists four configuration rows per project GUID. Synthetic 40 build-file rows: 2275 to 1272 tokens with short aliases; 12-project .sln matrix: 2304 to 486. Edits must use the saved original.
  - Test: Run on a real pbxproj and .sln; check a reader can answer which target builds X.swift; confirm edit anchors from the saved original still apply.
- **#1161** ★ [R] _medium_ **CloudTrail and cloud audit events to one-line digests** — Each CloudTrail event repeats userIdentity.sessionContext, tlsDetails, additionalEventData, requestID, eventID and recipientAccountId. Emit time, principal, action, resource, error code per event; keep full JSON for denied or failed calls. Synthetic S3 GetObject record: 502 vs 36 o200k tokens.
  - Test: Fetch 50 events with aws cloudtrail lookup-events; digest; ask who deleted resource X and from which IP; check AccessDenied events keep every field.
- **#1162** ★ [R] _medium_ **OTLP logs and metrics JSON to logfmt rows** — Collector file-exporter JSON carries timeUnixNano plus observedTimeUnixNano, severityNumber beside severityText, empty traceId, typed attribute wrappers and startTimeUnixNano per datapoint. Emit logfmt per log record and a table per metric. One synthetic log record: 129 vs 34 tokens. Sibling of #421.
  - Test: Run an OTel collector with the file exporter for logs and metrics; convert; assert counts and severities match; ask which service logged the most errors.
- **#1167** [L] _small_ **Decode JSON arrays of byte values to text** — journalctl -o json emits MESSAGE as an int array when text has ANSI or non-UTF-8 bytes; Node Buffer.toJSON gives {type:'Buffer',data:[...]}. Valid UTF-8 byte lists become strings. Synthetic 36-byte message: 77 vs 19 tokens. #325 and #619 cover base64 and bytes-repr only.
  - Test: Log a colored line via a systemd unit, read journalctl -o json; decode; assert re-encoding reproduces the original bytes; tokenise before and after.
- **#1168** ★ [R] _large_ **Flame graph SVG to folded stacks via rect nesting** — inferno, flamegraph.pl and py-spy SVGs store a rect (x, y, width, fill) plus title and text per frame. Rebuild stacks from y-depth and x-extent, emit 'a;b;c N' folded lines, then apply #422 and #423. #331 stubs SVG paths but ignores this.
  - Test: Generate an SVG with inferno from a perf sample; convert; check totals match the original folded input; ask for the hottest leaf and its callers.
- **#1169** [L] _small_ **Delta and run-length encode metric value lists** — Monotonic counters and flat plateaus in matrix values and series arrays. Print counters as first value plus deltas and repeats as 'v x N'. Synthetic 60 counters: 239 vs 124 tokens; 60 plateau values: 119 vs 9. #420 only handles regular timestamps.
  - Test: Query Prometheus counter series over 1h; encode; assert exact round trip; tokenise; ask peak delta and when values flatlined.
- **#1234** [L] _small_ **docker compose config: re-shorten long-form ports and volumes** — docker compose config expands ports, volumes and depends_on to long form (mode, target, published, protocol, create_host_path, condition, required). Rewrite to short syntax when no non-default sub-field exists, else keep long form. Synthetic service: 141 vs 67 o200k tokens.
  - Test: Run compose config on 5 real projects; feed each shortened output back through compose config and diff the resolved model against the original; compare tokens.
- **#1244** [R] _small_ `MCP` **Drop derivable selfLink, kind and fingerprint from gcloud, az** — gcloud compute describe YAML carries selfLink (a URL rebuilt from project, zone, name), kind: compute#..., fingerprint and labelFingerprint; az show repeats subscription and resource group inside id beside separate fields. A per-API derivable-field table drops them. Bash-side counterpart of MCP #131.
  - Test: Run gcloud compute instances describe and az vm show on one resource; count tokens in the listed fields; confirm they can be rebuilt from the kept fields.
- **#1245** [L] _small_ **Drop redundant xmlns redeclarations on child elements** — .NET XmlSerializer and SOAP stacks can repeat the same default xmlns on every nested element. Remove declarations that equal the in-scope binding; the infoset is unchanged. Synthetic 10 orders, three redeclarations each: 580 vs 280 o200k tokens.
  - Test: Canonicalise (C14N) before and after with lxml and compare bytes; count tokens on a captured SOAP response or WSDL-generated client log.
- **#1246** [R] _small_ **Peel SOAP envelopes and prefix noise from XML reads** — When no Fault is present, drop soap:Envelope, empty Header and Body wrappers and ns2:-style prefixes, printing the prefix-URI map once. Synthetic 5 responses: 326 vs 141 o200k tokens. Differs from #40 report stripping and #525 whitespace.
  - Test: Run against saved SOAP captures and a Fault case; check a reader answers field-lookup questions equally and Faults pass through untouched.
- **#1247** [R] **Render XSD and WSDL as type-signature lines** — xs:element, complexType and wsdl:message markup repeats name, type and minOccurs attributes with closing tags. Emit 'Order { id: int, total?: decimal }' and 'op GetOrder(req) -> resp' lines, like #521 does for GraphQL and JSON Schema.
  - Test: Convert a public WSDL with xsd imports, count o200k tokens both ways, then ask a reader which fields are optional or repeated.
- **#1248** [L] _small_ **Column-oriented dict-of-dicts (pandas to_json) to table** — df.to_json() and df.to_dict() default to {column: {index: value}}, repeating every index key in every column. When all columns share one index key set, emit TSV with an index column. Synthetic 3 columns x 40 rows: 527 vs 245 o200k tokens.
  - Test: Round-trip with pandas.read_json(orient='columns') versus parsing the TSV; compare frames. Also handle the Python repr form with int index keys.
- **#1249** [L] _small_ **Group sorted dotted-key listings by shared prefix** — git config -l, Spring env and configprops, .properties and flat_settings print the full dotted prefix on every line. Emit 'prefix: leaf=v leaf=v' blocks, exceptions inline. Synthetic: Spring keys 111 vs 83, git config 446 vs 407 o200k tokens. Generalises #516.
  - Test: Invert the grouping back to flat keys and compare for equality; run on git config --list --show-origin, application.properties and actuator /env captures.
- **#1250** [L] _tiny_ **Canonicalise ISO timestamps: drop .000 fractions, use Z** — JS toISOString, Jira and Python isoformat() emit '.000Z', '.000000+00:00' or '+0000'. Rewrite to 'Z' (same instant); unlike #258 nothing is truncated. 20 synthetic stamps: 319 or 399 vs 279 o200k tokens.
  - Test: Parse each stamp before and after with datetime and assert equal instants; only rewrite when every fraction in the output is zero. Measure on Jira and Graph MCP lists.
- **#1251** [R] _small_ **Strip comments from JSONC config reads (tsconfig, devcontainer)** — tsc --init writes a tsconfig dominated by commented-out options with trailing /* */ descriptions. Remove // and /* */ comments in .json files that use them, original saved. Synthetic tsc --init-style sample: 276 vs 49 o200k tokens. Not for files to be edited.
  - Test: Run tsc --init in a scratch dir, cat the file, count tokens before and after; parse stripped output with json5 and compare option values to the live keys.
- **#1253** [R] `MCP` **Detect HAL, JSON:API and OData link boilerplate by shape** — HAL _links, JSON:API links and relationships.*.links, and OData @odata.context/etag/id annotations can repeat per entity in Graph, SharePoint and hypermedia MCP servers. Detect the envelope shape, not the server name (#34); keep next-page links.
  - Test: Count token share of these keys in captured results from one OData, one HAL and one JSON:API server; check pagination still works from the retained next link.
- **#1254** [R] _small_ **Compact GraphQL error extensions: stack traces and tracing** — Apollo Server in non-production mode returns extensions.stacktrace arrays, and its tracing plugin adds per-resolver timings. Keep message, path, locations and extensions.code; reduce the stack to three frames, drop tracing. Complements #136, which unwraps data only.
  - Test: Fire a failing query at a local Apollo dev server via curl; count tokens of the error JSON before and after the rule.
- **#1255** [L] _tiny_ **Minify JSON leaves but keep one record per line** — Full minify (#31) yields one multi-kilobyte line, which per-line caps (#45), line-level selection (#87) and agent grep treat as a single unit. Put newlines only at depth 1-2 and minify below. Adds one newline per record.
  - Test: Feed fill-mode versus full-minified JSON through the line rules and classifier; compare tokens, truncation events and answer accuracy on lookup questions.
- **#1262** [R] _tiny_ `MCP` **Relative ages against the session date in MCP timestamps** — Rewrite *_at ISO stamps as ages from the date already in context ('4d', 'today 12:34') rather than date-only truncation (#258). o200k proxy: full stamp 13 tokens, date 6, '4d' 2. Original stays in the saved file.
  - Test: Tokenize synthetic list_issues-style items before and after (28-item set: 1204 vs 588 tokens); check a reader still answers oldest and newest questions.
- **#1321** [R] _small_ **Fold markers carry value counts and min/max of dropped rows** — Headroom's SmartCrusher reportedly keeps errors, outliers and boundary rows. Extend any row fold marker with per-column summaries of what was dropped: categorical counts such as status ok 40, failed 7, numeric min/max, date range. Aggregate questions then need no refetch.
  - Test: On JSON lists and tables, ask count, min, max and status questions over folded output with and without the summary; compare accuracy and refetch rate.
- **#1323** [R] _small_ **Contrast-based column selection: keep fields that separate failing rows** — Honeycomb BubbleUp compares anomalous and baseline rows to surface attributes whose distribution differs. For record lists with a failing subset (CI runs, pods, test results JSON), keep identifiers plus the top-lift fields, hoist the rest as constants.
  - Test: On kubectl and CI-run JSON with failing items, compute per-field lift, keep top 5, then ask why-failing questions; compare accuracy and tokens against full records.
- **#1326** ★ [L] _medium_ **Unicode box tables (DuckDB, polars, Keras, Rich) to TSV** — #412 covers only ASCII +---+ grids. DuckDB duckbox, polars frames, Keras 3 summary and Lightning ModelSummary draw box-drawing borders plus a dtype row. Emit TSV with dtypes folded into header cells. Hand-made 20-row duckbox: 468 vs 245 o200k tokens.
  - Test: Print the same 200-row frame via duckdb and polars; convert to TSV; round-trip cells against the source frame; compare o200k tokens.
- **#1329** ★ [R] _medium_ **Fold per-row-group column stats in Parquet and ORC metadata** — pyarrow ParquetFile.metadata, parquet-tools meta and orc-tool meta list every column chunk of every row group with codec, encodings, min/max and null counts (from memory). Collapse groups sharing a layout into one table plus overall min/max, rows and nulls; list anomalous groups. Unmeasured.
  - Test: Write a Parquet file with 50 row groups and 20 columns using pyarrow; print metadata; compare o200k tokens with the folded form; check codec and null-count questions remain answerable.
- **#1331** [R] _small_ **Drop spark.sql.sources.schema JSON that duplicates the column list** — SHOW CREATE TABLE, DESCRIBE FORMATTED and Glue get-table output for Spark-written tables carry spark.sql.sources.schema.part.N, JSON restating every column, plus Parquet serde class names and transient_lastDdlTime (from memory). Drop the JSON when it matches the columns; stub class names.
  - Test: Create a 30-column Parquet table in local PySpark with Hive support; run SHOW CREATE TABLE; compare o200k tokens with and without the property JSON; verify columns remain.
- **#1332** ★ [R] _medium_ **EXPLAIN FORMAT JSON plans to indented text without zero counters** — Postgres EXPLAIN (ANALYZE, BUFFERS, FORMAT JSON) emits about 25 keys per node, mostly false flags and zero Blocks counters; MySQL FORMAT=JSON and Mongo explain('executionStats') nest similarly. Render a text tree of nonzero fields. #883 covers text plans only. Hand-made node: 235 vs 52 tokens (o200k).
  - Test: Capture EXPLAIN (ANALYZE, BUFFERS, FORMAT JSON) of a 4-join query in local Postgres; convert; compare o200k tokens; check a reader still finds the slowest node and its row misestimate.
- **#1334** [R] _small_ **Fold passing checks in Great Expectations, pandera and Soda reports** — Great Expectations validation-result JSON repeats expectation_config, empty meta and null exception_info for every expectation, passing or not (from memory); Soda and dbt tests print per-check pass rows. Fold passes to a count; keep failures with unexpected_count and sample values. Unmeasured.
  - Test: Build a 40-expectation validation result with 3 failures in Great Expectations; compare o200k tokens raw versus folded; check failed expectation names and unexpected counts survive.
- **#1343** [R] _tiny_ **Drop long timezone names and monotonic tails from date strings** — JS Date.toString appends '(Central European Standard Time)' after GMT+0100 (23 versus 17 o200k tokens); Go time.Time String appends 'm=+0.012345678' (28 versus 19). Both tails are redundant or noise. Strip them, keep offset and instant.
  - Test: Grep saved Node, Go test and Java logs for GMT offsets followed by a zone name, and for ' m=+'; strip; verify parsed instants match; compare count_tokens.
- **#1345** [R] _tiny_ **Lowercase uppercase-hex runs in addresses, thumbprints and GUIDs** — Uppercase-hex producers (PowerShell Get-FileHash, keytool fingerprints) merge worse in BPE. Measured o200k proxy: 16-digit addresses 246 to 234 tokens, SHA-1 478 to 473, GUID and MAC about flat. Gate on 8+ digit runs; the saved original keeps the case.
  - Test: Generate matched upper and lower hex sets of 8 to 64 digits; count_tokens on Claude; if the gain is under 3% drop the idea, else grep a corpus.
- **#1365** [L] `MCP` **Round-trip-verified synthesised encoders for MCP and CLI output** — A coding model writes an encode/decode pair from about 10 captures of one tool; accept only if decode(encode(x)) equals x byte for byte on held-out captures and tokens drop. Lossless counterpart of lossy synthesis (#646); the decoder is only a proof.
  - Test: For 10 MCP tools or CLIs, synthesise from 10 captures; report held-out round-trip pass rate, tokens saved against hand-written columnar encoding (#32), and reader QA parity.

## Tool-specific noise packs

- **#46** [R] **pytest noise pack** — Drop platform/rootdir/plugins header, PASSED lines, repeated captured sections; keep failures and the short summary.
- **#47** [R] **jest, vitest, mocha noise pack** — Drop PASS lines; collapse coverage tables to uncovered files.
- **#48** [R] **npm, pnpm, yarn noise pack** — npm warn deprecated xN, funding/audit footers, progress.
- **#49** [R] **pip, uv, poetry noise pack** — Requirement already satisfied, Collecting, download bars.
- **#50** [R] **cargo, go, gradle, maven, make noise pack** — 'Compiling x v1.2.3' xN to a count plus the last one; '> Task :x UP-TO-DATE'; '[INFO] Downloading'.
- **#51** [R] **docker build/pull layer chatter** — Pulling fs layer, Waiting, Verifying Checksum, Pull complete.
- **#52** [R] **kubectl, terraform, helm noise pack** — managedFields, 'Refreshing state...' xN, 'Still creating... [10s elapsed]'.
- **#53** [R] **apt and brew noise pack** — Get:N, Reading package lists, Unpacking, Setting up.
- **#54** [L] **Linters and type checkers grouping** — Group diagnostics by rule: message once, then the file:line list.
- **#55** [R] **Compiler backtraces** — Keep first/last N lines of include stacks and 'in instantiation of' chains.
- **#56** [R] **Stack traces** — Collapse framework frames (site-packages, node_modules, java.base/, <frozen importlib>) to '... 14 framework frames'.
- **#57** [R] **Drop rarely used columns** — ls -l, ps, du, lsof; test whether the agent tolerates it.
- **#58** [R] `MCP` **SQL and DB MCP results** — Drop all-null columns, truncate blob columns.
- **#59** [X] `eval` **Pack-discovery tool** — Cluster lines across captured outputs by template and rank templates by total tokens; finds the next noise rules from data instead of guessing.
- **#118** ★ [R] _medium_ **Strip echoed source lines and caret markers from diagnostics** — Drop the echoed source line, gutter and ~~~/^^^ squiggles from ruff full format, tsc --pretty, esbuild and babel; file:line:col plus message suffices. Python 3.11+ traceback marker lines and difflib '?' lines go too. Measured: ruff 1763 vs 525 chars concise, tsc 1460 vs 298 plain.
  - Test: Run ruff and tsc in full and concise modes and collect 100 tracebacks; count tokens saved; replay fixes to check agents resolve errors from file:line:col alone.
- **#119** [R] _small_ **Drop pytest -vv Common items and Full diff sections** — In a -vv dict failure the repr appears in the assert line, Common items, Full diff and the FAILED summary line. Measured: 4940 chars total; Common items 642, Full diff 918, FAILED line 678. Keep Differing items and +/- lines only.
  - Test: Generate failing dict, list, str and dataclass asserts under -vv; tokenise each section; verify the +/- lines alone preserve every difference.
- **#121** ★ [R] _large_ **Lockfile and lockfile-diff digest without integrity hashes** — package-lock.json, yarn.lock, pnpm-lock.yaml, Cargo.lock, uv.lock and go.sum hunks become 'pkg old->new' lines; integrity hashes and resolved URLs dropped. Here, 823k of uv.lock's 858k chars sit on sha256 lines. Needs a lockfile exception to the git diff skip.
  - Test: Take git diff for 20 lockfile-touching commits; compare tokens raw vs digest by line class; try :(exclude) lockfile pathspecs in PreToolUse; check agents still name changed versions.
- **#124** ★ [R] _medium_ **Rails, Django and Alembic log pack: one line per request** — Rails logs Started, Processing, Parameters, SQL lines with caller arrows, Rendered timings and Completed (from memory); Django logs 'Not Found' then the access line. Emit one line per request. Django migrate printed 18 'Applying... OK' lines of 21; collapse OK runs and banners, keep failures.
  - Test: Drive 50 requests and run migrate, db:migrate and alembic upgrade on sample apps; compare raw vs collapsed tokens; check the slow or failing request is still findable.
- **#125** ★ [R] _medium_ **Webpack, Vite and Next build report digest** — Webpack default stats list every module with [built] flags and nested 'modules by path' groups (from memory); Next.js prints a route-size table plus a constant legend. Keep warnings, errors, top assets by size and totals. A tiny vite build was only 4 lines.
  - Test: Build a 300-module webpack app and a Next.js app; classify lines by template; check which lines agents ever act on versus errors and warnings.
- **#127** ★ [R] _medium_ **One line per finding for npm audit, SARIF and scanners** — npm audit text prints a title plus GHSA URL per advisory (handlebars 4.0.5 gave 20 such lines in 4,915 chars). For it and semgrep/CodeQL SARIF, trivy, grype, pip-audit, emit 'pkg version advisory fixed-in' or 'file:line rule message'; drop fingerprints, URL arrays, CVSS vectors.
  - Test: Run npm audit and semgrep --sarif on sample repos; compare digest tokens; verify every file:line and fixed-in version survives and the agent picks the right fix command.
- **#137** ★ [R] _medium_ **Stub kubectl last-applied-configuration annotation** — kubectl get -o yaml/json embeds the whole object again as a JSON string in the kubectl.kubernetes.io/last-applied-configuration annotation. Replace it with a stub; the original stays in the saved file. Separate from managedFields and status noise.
  - Test: Run kubectl get deploy -o yaml on a client-side-applied object; measure the annotation's share of tokens; check agents still answer spec questions.
- **#214** ★ [R] _large_ **xcodebuild: drop per-step cd and compiler command lines** — Each xcodebuild step header (SwiftCompile, Ld, CodeSign, ProcessInfoPlistFile) is reportedly followed by an indented cd line and a full tool command with long flag lists (from memory, unmeasured). Keep headers, warnings and errors; the saved original keeps the commands.
  - Test: Capture xcodebuild logs from CI or a Mac; classify lines as header, command or diagnostic; compare tokens; check an agent still fixes a compile error from what remains.
- **#215** [R] _small_ **XCTest and swift test: fold started/passed pairs and suites** — XCTest reportedly prints 'Test Case ... started.' then 'passed (0.001 seconds).', plus timestamped 'Test Suite' started/passed lines at class, bundle and 'All tests' levels, each followed by an 'Executed N tests' line (from memory). Keep failures and the final tally.
  - Test: Run swift test or xcodebuild test on a package with 50 tests; count started lines, suite banners and Executed lines; verify failing case names and file:line survive.
- **#216** ★ [R] _medium_ **MSBuild: dedupe warnings repeated in summary and per framework** — dotnet build reportedly prints each warning and error inline and again in the end summary, every line ending in [/abs/path/proj.csproj], repeated per target framework (from memory). Key on file, position, code, message; fold repeats to one line plus count; hoist the project path.
  - Test: Build a multi-targeting sample solution with 20 warnings (CI logs if dotnet is absent); measure the duplicate-diagnostic char share; confirm every code and file:line survives.
- **#218** ★ [R] _medium_ **go test -v: drop RUN/PAUSE/CONT lines with no interleaved output** — Measured: a 4-subtest parallel Go test printed 13 '=== RUN/PAUSE/CONT' lines out of 18. Drop them when the matching '--- PASS/FAIL' line exists and nothing sits between; keep any '=== NAME' that attributes streamed t.Log output.
  - Test: Run go test -v on parallel subtests, with and without t.Log in failing cases; count dropped lines; check each log line stays attributable to its test.
- **#220** ★ [R] _large_ **g++/clang overload errors: cap candidate notes, keep first three** — Measured: one missing operator<< for a struct printed 20.8k chars, 76% of it 'note: candidate' and 'no known conversion' blocks with echoed code. Keep the error plus the first few candidates and '+N more'; the rest stays in the saved file.
  - Test: Collect 20 g++ and clang template or overload errors; measure candidate-note share; check an agent still fixes each error from the top three candidates plus the count.
- **#221** ★ [R] _medium_ **ASan/UBSan/Valgrind: drop shadow-byte dumps, hoist ==PID== prefix** — Measured: a 4-byte heap overflow under ASan gave 2,958 chars, 1,405 of them the constant shadow-byte legend and memory dump after the stack frames. Valgrind prefixes every line with ==PID==. Keep error line, stacks, allocation sites; save the rest.
  - Test: Run ASan, TSan and Valgrind on 20 small buggy programs; compare tokens; verify the faulting source line and allocation site remain in the trimmed report.
- **#222** [R] _small_ **CMake configure: fold probe/'- done' pairs, drop percent prefixes** — A trivial project's configure printed 4 of 12 lines as 'X' then 'X - done/Success/skipped' pairs; find_package-heavy projects print many more. Fold successful pairs to one line, keep failed and not-found probes. Make's '[ 37%]' prefix adds no information.
  - Test: Configure and build two real C++ projects; count probe pairs and percent-prefix tokens against total; confirm failed probes and warnings pass through verbatim.
- **#223** ★ [R] _medium_ **gradle dependencies: print identical configuration trees once** — gradle dependencies prints dozens of configurations (compileClasspath, runtimeClasspath, test variants, annotationProcessor) whose trees mostly repeat, with (*) and (c) markers (from memory). Hash each block; print distinct trees once with the configuration names sharing them.
  - Test: Run gradle dependencies on an Android or multi-module sample; hash configuration blocks; compute duplicate share; ask which version of X is on runtimeClasspath from the folded form.
- **#224** ★ [R] _medium_ **Maven reactor stanzas and Gradle/Maven failure footers** — Per module Maven prints a banner, 'Building', '--- plugin:version:goal @ module ---' and 'Nothing to compile'; failures end with constant '-e/-X' advice and [Help 1] URLs. Gradle ends with '* Try:' and deprecation-advisory paragraphs. Replace exact paragraphs with a tag; keep Reactor Summary.
  - Test: Run mvn verify on a 10-module project and a failing gradle build; extract exact repeated paragraphs; count tokens per build; check agents still find the failing module and goal.
- **#225** [R] _small_ **Collapse zero-test harness blocks: cargo doc-tests, go no-test-files** — Measured: a 3-crate cargo workspace printed a 'Doc-tests x / running 0 tests / test result: ok. 0 passed...' block per crate; Running lines end in a build hash. Fold to 'N targets, 0 tests'. Same for go '[no test files]' lines.
  - Test: Generate a 40-crate cargo workspace offline with cargo new; run cargo test; count tokens in zero-test blocks versus total; run go test ./... on a multi-package module.
- **#229** [L] _small_ **Symbolic file modes to octal in ls -l** — Mode strings like -rw-r--r-- tokenize as many symbol pieces while 644 is one or two tokens. o200k proxy: a 30-line listing went 700 to 560 tokens. Use four digits for setuid and sticky, keep the ACL plus or at suffix. Also find -ls, tar -tv.
  - Test: Tokenize ls -lA of /usr/bin and node_modules before and after; assert octal-to-symbolic round trip equals the original on every line; check the agent still answers executable-bit questions.
- **#236** [R] _small_ **Collapse parallel-locale catalogs to source language** — grep -r of one key across locales/, .po or messages.*.json repeats a string in 30 languages. Rare scripts cost more tokens: a translated pangram was 15 tokens in Arabic versus 9 in English (o200k proxy). Keep the source locale, list the others by code.
  - Test: Grep a key across a repo with 20+ locales; measure token share by script; collapse non-source locales; check the agent can still locate and edit a given locale.
- **#261** [M] _small_ **Distill model drop decisions into per-command line-template tables** — Run the classifier over many captured outputs of one command family, mask digits and hex, and keep line templates it drops in at least 95% of occurrences. Result is a deterministic, auditable drop table needing no model load, built from the user's own traffic.
  - Test: Mine tables from 150 saved pytest, npm and cargo outputs; apply to 50 held-out; compare chars removed and needle recall against the model at 0.5.
- **#308** [R] _small_ **Match-count histogram instead of hits for over-broad searches** — When rg/grep returns over ~60 lines, emit per-file counts for the top 25 files, 3 sample hits each and a 'narrow the pattern' hint; original saved. SWE-agent's search commands reportedly refuse past 50 matches and ask for refinement rather than listing.
  - Test: Replay 50 recorded large grep outputs; count tokens saved, and how often the next call was a narrower grep versus a read of the saved file.
- **#310** ★ [R] _medium_ **Cloud CLI --help: keep flag headers plus first paragraph** — Measured: gcloud compute instances create --help is 14,272 o200k tokens, 12,351 in FLAGS, and SYNOPSIS re-lists every flag. Keeping each flag header plus first paragraph left 47%. az -h and aws help likely similar.
  - Test: Digest gcloud, az, aws and kubectl help for 5 commands each; ask 10 flag-usage questions per command; compare answer accuracy and tokens against full help.
- **#311** ★ [R] _medium_ **GitHub Actions logs: fold passing steps, keep failing step** — gh run view --log and get_job_logs prefix each line with a 28-char timestamp (18 o200k tokens versus 11 for a sample line itself) and dump with:/env: inputs per step. Fold ##[group] steps without error or warning to name, duration and line count.
  - Test: Pull 10 failed-run logs with gh run view --log; fold green steps; assert root-cause error lines survive; ask which step failed and why, versus raw; compare tokens.
- **#312** ★ [R] _medium_ **BuildKit plain progress: regroup steps, drop download ticks** — docker build --progress=plain prefixes lines with '#N 1.842' elapsed times, interleaves parallel steps, and reportedly prints sha256 download ticks (3 synthetic ticks: 240 of 326 tokens). Regroup by step id; keep only the final tick, CACHED, DONE and errors.
  - Test: Verify that plain progress prints download ticks: build a multi-stage Dockerfile cold and warm; count tick and prefix tokens; confirm RUN error output and exit code survive regrouping.
- **#313** ★ [R] _medium_ **Terraform plan: fold known-after-apply lines and tags_all copies** — New resources list many '+ attr = (known after apply)' lines, and the AWS provider's tags_all repeats tags. Fold computed attributes into one 'computed: a, b, c' line; mark tags_all 'same as tags'. Synthetic block: 112 tokens to 39.
  - Test: Collect 5 plan outputs (docs samples or terraform_data); count computed and tags_all lines; fold; ask which attributes change and which are computed, versus raw plan.
- **#314** ★ [R] _large_ **terraform show -json plan: keep only changed resources** — Plan JSON reportedly stores attributes several times: planned_values, resource_changes before/after, after_unknown, sensitive maps, prior_state, configuration.
