Keep non-no-op changes as changed-attribute before/after pairs; the full JSON stays in the saved original.
  - Test: Run terraform show -json on 5 plans; measure byte share per top-level key; build the digest; ask what is created, changed or destroyed and which attributes differ.
- **#317** [R] _small_ **kubectl describe and pod YAML: drop <none> lines, runtime IDs** — describe prints '<none>' fields, default NoExecute tolerations and the projected kube-api-access volume (124 tokens synthetic). Pod YAML status holds containerID and imageID hex digests (39 and 45 tokens each), likely force-kept by the classifier. Stub these; keep events and conditions.
  - Test: Capture describe pod and get pod -o yaml from a kind cluster, including a CrashLoopBackOff pod; tokenize dropped classes; check an agent still diagnoses the crash.
- **#318** ★ [R] _medium_ **ansible-playbook: per-task host counts, failures verbatim** — Each task prints 'ok: [host]' or 'skipping: [host]' per host under asterisk-padded headers. Fold to 'TASK name: ok 48, changed 2, skipped 0', listing only changed, failed or unreachable hosts; keep PLAY RECAP and failure result dicts verbatim.
  - Test: Run ansible-playbook over 50 connection=local inventory hosts with one failing task; count host-line tokens; confirm failed and unreachable lines plus recap survive verbatim.
- **#326** [R] _small_ **Strip template comments, placeholders and bot blobs from issue bodies** — Issue and PR bodies carry template HTML comments, '_No response_' stubs, empty headings, badge images and bot blocks (Dependabot commands footers with release notes, coverage or preview-deploy tables). Synthetic template: 72 vs 8 tokens. Keep human prose and checked items.
  - Test: Pull 50 issue and PR bodies, including 10 Dependabot PRs, via gh or MCP; measure token share of comments, placeholders and bot blocks; ask reproduce-steps questions on both versions.
- **#355** [R] _small_ **Prune Android uiautomator and Appium XML hierarchies** — uiautomator dump nodes carry about 15 attributes, mostly false or empty (checkable, long-clickable, password). Drop default-valued attributes, shorten class and package names, unwrap anonymous containers lacking text, id or interaction. Synthetic node: 89 to 16 tokens. Appium page source shares the shape.
  - Test: Dump hierarchies from an emulator on 5 screens; tokenize raw versus pruned; check an agent still finds tap targets by bounds and resource-id.
- **#391** [R] _small_ **Rewrite default git log entries to one line each** — Verified here on an o200k proxy: 21 commits cost 2285 tokens in default format, 596 as 'short-sha date author subject', 386 as --oneline. The hook does not skip plain git log. Parse commit/Author/Date blocks, keep body lines indented, save original.
  - Test: Parse git log, reflog and shortlog output from five repos; reformat; assert each short sha resolves uniquely; ask who/when/what questions against both forms.
- **#393** [R] _small_ **env, printenv and set pack: drop noise vars, mask secrets** — Verified here: env output was 97% protected by character share, so the classifier cannot shrink it. Rules can: drop LS_COLORS, LESSOPEN, XDG_*, SSH_AUTH_SOCK-style vars, dedupe PATH entries, and mask token-like values, which doubles as a leak guard.
  - Test: Capture env and set from five dev containers and CI shells; apply drop and mask lists; tokenize; ask PATH, HOME, VIRTUAL_ENV and proxy questions; grep masked output for secrets.
- **#396** ★ [R] _medium_ **Strip quoted replies and signatures from email and ticket threads** — Each reply in a mail or ticket thread re-quotes everything before it, so tokens grow quadratically ('>' prefixed lines, 'On <date> <name> wrote:' headers). Drop '>' blocks whose text already appears in an earlier message, plus signature and disclaimer footers.
  - Test: Fetch 10 long threads through an email or ticket MCP; strip quote blocks present earlier plus footers; tokenize; ask who decided what, and when, against the original.
- **#398** [R] _tiny_ **Drop +/- bar graphs from git diff --stat output** — Verified here: 118 stat lines over 30 commits cost 1901 o200k-proxy tokens with bars, 1739 without. The count column already carries size; the bar adds only a scaled plus/minus split.
  - Test: Regex-strip bars from git diff --stat, log --stat and show --stat; assert file names and counts are unchanged on 20 repos; tokenize before and after.
- **#406** [R] _tiny_ **Drop repeated WebSearch REMINDER footer after first result** — All 15 WebSearch results in local transcripts end with the identical 'REMINDER: You MUST include the sources...' line, about 20 tokens each (my estimate). Keep it on the first result per session, drop later copies via updatedToolOutput.
  - Test: Verify that updatedToolOutput works for WebSearch (item 72); count footer repeats per session; check the model still ends answers with a Sources list when later footers are dropped.
- **#409** [R] _small_ **Fold the dominant value in df.info() and isnull().sum()** — df.info() prints 'N non-null' and a dtype per column; isnull().sum() prints a 0 per column. Print only exceptions plus 'all other columns: 200 non-null'. 18-column frame: info() 334 tokens, isnull().sum() 117 (o200k). Same for nunique, dtypes.
  - Test: Run the four calls on 10 real datasets; tokenise raw versus folded; ask 'which columns have nulls' and 'which dtype is column X' on both.
- **#413** ★ [R] _medium_ **Spark driver INFO logs: fold per-task lines into stage summary** — spark-submit at INFO logs a Starting-task and Finished-task line per task, plus ResourceUtils, BlockManager and MemoryStore chatter (from memory). Fold to per-stage task counts and slowest task; keep WARN, ERROR and stack traces verbatim.
  - Test: Run a local[4] pyspark job over 200 partitions at INFO; classify lines by template; rank templates by tokens; confirm a failing-task line survives.
- **#414** [R] _small_ **Spark explain: strip #exprId suffixes and repeated plan phases** — Spark plans suffix each attribute with a session-unique id (id#12L), and explain(True) prints parsed, analyzed, optimized and physical plans (from memory). Strip ids; keep optimized plus physical. Three-line synthetic plan: 71 vs 45 tokens (o200k).
  - Test: Run explain(True) on a join plus aggregate in local pyspark; tokenise; check the scan filter and join strategy are still identifiable from the reduced plan.
- **#415** ★ [R] _medium_ **pg_dump and mysqldump boilerplate; one-row INSERTs to multi-row** — Drop the pg_dump SET preamble, OWNER TO lines and '-- Name: x; Type:' banners, and mysqldump '/*!40101 */' pragmas; hoist repeated ENGINE/CHARSET clauses; merge per-row INSERTs sharing a column list into one VALUES list (from memory).
  - Test: Dump a sample Postgres and MySQL schema with data; classify lines by template; tokenise before and after; check table and column names all survive.
- **#416** ★ [R] _medium_ **ML framework startup and warning boilerplate pack** — Import and train-start lines are fixed text: TensorFlow cpu_feature_guard, cuDNN factory registration and TensorRT notices; HF 'Some weights were not initialized'; tokenizers fork warning; Lightning GPU/TPU/HPU lines; sklearn ConvergenceWarning advice with doc URLs (from memory).
  - Test: Run small torch, TF, transformers and sklearn scripts in a clean env; extract constant lines with the pack-discovery tool (#59); keep warnings naming user code.
- **#419** ★ [R] _medium_ **Prometheus /metrics exposition: hoist labels, fold zero series** — Scrapes repeat metric name and full label set on every bucket and series line, plus HELP/TYPE lines. Drop HELP, hoist shared labels per family, fold zero-valued series and empty histogram buckets into counts. One 3-label bucket line is 26 tokens (o200k proxy).
  - Test: curl /metrics from a Go or Node service; tokenise per line class (HELP, bucket, zero value); compare raw versus folded; ask 5 'is metric X nonzero' questions on both.
- **#422** ★ [R] _medium_ **Profiler outputs: aggregate, then cut rows by cumulative weight** — cProfile tables, jmap -histo, perf script samples, .cpuprofile and Chrome trace-event JSON list hundreds of rows or raw events. Aggregate self-time per function, keep rows to 95% of total weight, add a residual row. One trace event: 81 tokens, summary line 8 (o200k proxy).
  - Test: Profile a small Python or Node program with cProfile and --cpu-prof; aggregate; tokenise; ask for the top 3 hot functions and total time from both forms.
- **#426** [R] _small_ **Strip addresses, symbol hashes and arg words from stack frames** — Rust '::h<16 hex>' symbol hashes, Go '(0xc000..., 0x1)' argument words and '+0x1d' offsets, gdb '0x00007f... in', Python 'object at 0x7f...' and Java '$$Lambda$N/0x...' are noise to the agent. Synthetic frame tokens (o200k proxy): Go 44 to 9, gdb 75 to 38.
  - Test: Collect backtraces from Rust (RUST_BACKTRACE=1), Go panic, gdb bt, Python and Java; strip; count tokens; check the agent can still locate the failing file:line and function.
- **#427** ★ [R] _medium_ **Crash-report section triage: drop libraries, memory maps, register dumps** — JVM hs_err_pid files, Android tombstones, macOS crash reports and Linux oops print long sections (Dynamic libraries, Environment Variables, memory maps, Binary Images, 'Modules linked in', 'Code:' bytes) after the cause. Keep header, signal, crashing thread; stub the rest with line counts.
  - Test: Trigger crashes (Unsafe on a JVM, ctypes null deref with faulthandler, a segfaulting C binary); measure token share per section; verify cause and top frames remain.
- **#428** [R] _small_ **Zero-valued counter dumps: ethtool -S, procfs, smaps** — Dumps like ethtool -S (per-queue counters), /proc/PID/smaps, /proc/vmstat, redis INFO and cgroup stat files list many 'key: 0' lines. Drop them, print 'N zero counters omitted', keep nonzero. Five zero smaps lines cost 44 tokens (o200k proxy).
  - Test: Capture ethtool -S, /proc/self/smaps and /proc/vmstat on a real box; tokenise the zero-valued share; ask 'which counters are nonzero' and 'is X zero' on both forms.
- **#479** [R] _small_ **Keep only each bot's latest comment in PR threads** — Some bots (coverage, preview deploys, review summaries) post a fresh comment on each push, and walkthrough comments carry details blocks and mermaid diagrams. For user.type Bot or [bot] logins keep only each login's newest comment, with details and diagrams stripped; save the rest.
  - Test: Fetch comments for 20 PRs with active bots via MCP; measure bot share of tokens; fold; ask 'is coverage dropping, what do humans still need to change' versus raw.
- **#498** ★ [R] _medium_ **Show lint and typecheck findings only in git-diff-touched files** — Reviewdog's filter modes and diff-quality reportedly report only findings on added lines. For ruff, mypy, tsc, eslint keep findings in files git diff touched, plus a count and first three of the rest. Needs no earlier run, unlike #141 and #142.
  - Test: Repo with 200 old findings; edit 3 files, one breaking a caller elsewhere; compare output size and confirm the cross-file break still appears in the outside-diff summary.
- **#508** ★ [R] _medium_ **strace: collapse loader preamble, join unfinished/resumed pairs** — In strace -f ls /tmp, 3,050 of 3,436 o200k tokens preceded the first write: execve, ld.so, libc, locale, mmap, mprotect. Collapse successful startup syscalls to one line; keep failures (= -1 E...), connects, non-library opens, signals, exit. Join <unfinished>/<resumed> pairs.
  - Test: Run strace -f on ls, python -c 'import json' and curl; compare tokens; assert every ENOENT/EACCES line, connect and exit_group survives.
- **#509** [R] _small_ **dmesg: fold OOM tables, audit denials, boot-time chatter** — Keep Oops, BUG, WARN and error lines verbatim. Cut OOM-killer Mem-Info and task tables to the victim line plus top RSS rows. Fold repeated apparmor/audit DENIED lines to counts per profile, operation and name. Collapse boot runs of ACPI, pci, virtio lines.
  - Test: Capture dmesg from a fresh boot, a cgroup OOM kill and a denied apparmor profile; compare tokens; verify every error/warning line and the killed process name survive.
- **#511** [R] _small_ **systemctl list-units and status: drop legend, fold healthy units** — From memory: list-units ends with a LOAD/ACTIVE/SUB legend and lists dozens of healthy .device, .mount, .slice and .scope rows; status prints CGroup trees of full command lines. Drop the legend, fold healthy rows to per-type counts, keep failed and not-found, cap command lines.
  - Test: On a systemd host capture list-units --all, --failed and status of a busy service; compare tokens; check failed and not-found units and the main PID remain.
- **#512** [R] _small_ **nmap: drop verbose duplicates, fold identical-host sweeps** — From memory: -v prints 'Discovered open port' lines repeating the final table, Initiating/Completed/Stats progress lines, a data-files path, and 'Service detection performed' and 'Raw packets sent' footers. Drop them; fold hosts with identical port signatures; in -oG drop 'Status: Up' lines.
  - Test: Scan localhost and a /24 lab with nmap -v -sV and -oG; compare tokens; check open-port, version and host-up answers match between raw and folded outputs.
- **#513** [R] _small_ **openssl x509 -text and s_client: digest certificates** — x509 -text prints colon-hex modulus, signature and SCT blocks; s_client -showcerts prints full PEM chains, session ticket and Master-Key. Keep subject, issuer, validity, SANs, key and signature algorithm, verify result; replace PEM and hex blocks with SHA-256 fingerprint stubs.
  - Test: Run s_client -showcerts against a public host and x509 -text on its leaf; compare tokens; ask expiry date, SANs and whether verification succeeded on both forms.
- **#514** [R] _small_ **ps aux: fold kernel threads, hash-cap very long argv** — Measured here: 70 of 83 ps aux rows were bracketed kernel threads (kworker, ksoftirqd, rcu_*), about 2,950 of 21,530 o200k tokens. Fold to 'N kernel threads: kworker x30, ...'; cap argv beyond a length with a hash; keep user processes verbatim.
  - Test: Run ps aux and ps -ef on a VM and in a container; compare tokens; ask which PID runs a given script and how many kernel threads exist.
- **#600** [R] _small_ **pytest tracebacks: trim echoed test-body source above '>' line** — Default pytest failures echo the whole test function above the '>' line. Keep the def line, three lines before '>' and the E lines. Measured: 12 parametrized failures cost 1,868 tokens under --tb=auto, 968 under --tb=short (o200k proxy). Alternative: PYTEST_ADDOPTS in settings env.
  - Test: Run pytest on 20 long-bodied failing tests; trim; tokenise; check each failing assert and fixture value stays identifiable from def line, context and E lines.
- **#602** [R] _small_ **Playwright: fold retry attempts and Call log polling cycles** — Playwright reportedly reprints the full error per retry attempt, and its Call log repeats 'waiting for locator / element not stable / retrying click action' cycles with growing waits. Fold identical attempts to 'Retry #1 identical' and repeated cycles to a count.
  - Test: Verify that retries reprint the error: run a flaky spec with retries=2 and a hidden button; fold; assert first cycle, final timeout and locator survive; compare tokens.
- **#603** [L] _tiny_ **Jest console.log capture stanzas to one line** — Jest prints each captured console call as a label line, blank line, indented message, blank line and 'at file:line:col' line (from memory). Rewrite to 'console.log src/a.test.ts:12: message' and fold identical repeats with counts. Same facts, re-encoded.
  - Test: Run a jest suite with 100 debug prints; rewrite stanzas; tokenise before and after; ask which test printed value X from the rewritten form.
- **#604** ★ [R] _medium_ **tsc --traceResolution and --listFiles: fold successful resolutions** — Measured: a 2-file project's --traceResolution output is 19.6k chars (4,886 o200k-proxy tokens) against 370 chars for plain tsc. Fold each Resolving block ending 'successfully resolved' to one line, keep unresolved blocks whole, collapse lib and @types entries in --listFiles to counts.
  - Test: Run tsc --traceResolution on a 200-import project with two broken imports; fold; assert failed blocks stay whole; compare tokens; ask why module X fails.
- **#605** [R] _small_ **TypeScript error elaboration chains: keep head and deepest lines** — TS2322 and TS2345 errors print nested 'not assignable / types of property are incompatible' lines, often with huge inlined types. Keyed on indentation, keep the first line, the deepest two lines and an 'N intermediate lines' marker. C++ sibling is #220.
  - Test: Collect tsc errors from a Prisma, zod or react-query heavy repo; fold chains; check the deepest lines name the root cause; replay fixes from folded versus full.
- **#607** [R] _small_ **Collapse applied migrations in showmigrations and db:migrate:status** — Django showmigrations prints '[X] 0001_initial' per migration per app, contrib apps alone adding dozens; Rails db:migrate:status, knex and prisma status list every applied row. Fold fully applied apps to 'auth: 12 applied' and show unapplied rows. Distinct from #124.
  - Test: Run showmigrations on a Django project with 15 apps and db:migrate:status on a Rails app; fold; ask which migrations are pending; compare tokens.
- **#608** [R] _small_ **rails routes and Django show_urls: drop framework-internal rows** — rails routes in a default app lists ActiveStorage, ActionMailbox and rails/info routes with long signed-blob paths, and blanks Prefix on repeat rows (from memory). Replace framework-owned controller rows with a count; keep app routes.
  - Test: Run rails routes on 3 apps and show_urls on a Django project; drop internals; check an agent still finds the controller for a given path; count tokens.
- **#609** ★ [R] _medium_ **Python lock hashes: requirements --hash, poetry.lock, Pipfile.lock** — Python counterpart to #121: pip-compile --generate-hashes puts several --hash=sha256 continuation lines on every pin, poetry.lock lists a file and hash per wheel, Pipfile.lock embeds hashes, and pip-compile adds '# via' blocks. Collapse each package to 'name==version'.
  - Test: Take pinned Python lockfiles from 10 repos; strip hash and via lines; assert name==version pairs match a pip parse of the original; compare tokens.
- **#611** [R] _small_ **Constant banners: ELIFECYCLE footers, setuptools star-bars, Sass deprecations** — Signature-fold recurring blocks: 'npm ERR! code ELIFECYCLE' footers and yarn 'Visit docs' lines, setuptools banners fenced by 80-asterisk bars, and Sass deprecation blocks with box-drawn code frames. Keep the first line plus URL; fold repeats per deprecation id with counts.
  - Test: Collect failing npm test, pip install -e and sass runs; fold blocks; assert the first message and exit status survive; tokenise before and after.
- **#618** [L] _tiny_ **Canonicalise zero-padded hex words and IPv6 addresses** — Fixed-width 0x0000000000000001 values (gdb registers, lldb, Rust {:#018x}) and full IPv6 text cost digit-group tokens. Synthetic: register line 33 vs 22, IPv6 24 vs 13 (RFC 5952 form). Value-preserving; skip hexdump columns.
  - Test: Run gdb info registers, ip -6 addr, ss -6; rewrite via int() and ipaddress compressed form; assert numeric equality; tokenise; check agent greps for full addresses still work.
- **#646** ★ [M] _medium_ **LLM-synthesised per-command filter programs, validated by replay** — Give a coding model about 10 captured outputs of one command family; it writes a small Python filter. Accept only if held-out replays keep every needle line (errors, failing names) and drop 30%+ of chars. Runs without the model; unlike #261, authored rather than mined.
  - Test: For npm install, cargo build and pytest, synthesise from 10 runs, test on 10 held-out; report chars removed, needle recall, and QA against classifier at 0.5.
- **#693** [R] _small_ **Strip JAVA_TOOL_OPTIONS 'Picked up' banner from JVM launches** — Every java, javac, mvn and gradle launch prints 'Picked up JAVA_TOOL_OPTIONS: ...' on stderr, and forked daemons and surefire JVMs repeat it. Measured here: 788 chars, 303 o200k tokens per launch. Drop every copy and keep other warnings.
  - Test: Run java -version, mvn -v and a 5-module mvn test with the variable set; count banner copies and tokens; strip; confirm exit codes and real warnings are untouched.
- **#696** [R] _small_ **Drop expected/actual dumps when a diff follows** — Go testify prints expected, actual, then a Diff block; Rust pretty_assertions and gtest 'Which is:' lines add similar duplicates (from memory). Where a diff exists, keep it and elide the full dumps.
  - Test: Fail assert.Equal on a 60-field struct and Rust assert_eq on nested Vecs with {:#?}; compare tokens; check the differing field stays identifiable.
- **#700** [R] _tiny_ **Strip Version, Culture, PublicKeyToken tails from .NET type names** — Assembly-qualified names in .NET exceptions, serializer and DI errors repeat ', Version=8.0.0.0, Culture=neutral, PublicKeyToken=b77a5c561934e089' after each type, often nested in generic brackets (from memory). Strip the tail, keeping the version only for the assembly a load failure names.
  - Test: Collect 20 .NET exceptions with generic types plus FileLoadException cases; count tokens in the tails; strip; confirm a version mismatch can still be diagnosed.
- **#701** ★ [R] _medium_ **Ninja and Bazel failures: hoist repeated compiler command lines** — After each 'FAILED: target', ninja prints the full compiler command (Bazel prints one too, reportedly). Measured: in a 2-file failing CMake build, commands were 278 of 674 o200k tokens, the second repeating the first's flags. Print compiler plus source and hoist shared flags once.
  - Test: Break 5 files in a CMake project with many -I and -D flags; build with ninja -k 0; compare tokens; confirm the saved original keeps the exact rerun command.
- **#702** [R] _small_ **Fold expanded libstdc++ type names to std::string-style aliases** — Linker, ASan, Valgrind and gdb print expanded names like std::__cxx11::basic_string<char, std::char_traits<char>, std::allocator<char> >. A fixed table (string, default allocator, less, char_traits arguments) shortens them; a partial regex pass took one linker error from 182 to 93 o200k tokens.
  - Test: Collect 20 linker errors and ASan/Valgrind traces from STL-heavy code; apply the table; check distinct symbols never collapse together and agents still locate the missing definition.
- **#703** ★ [R] _large_ **Drop system-header regions from gcc -E and clang -E output** — Preprocessor output marks regions with linemarkers like '# 12 "/usr/include/..." 3'; flag 3 means system header. Measured: including <iostream> and <vector> gave 291k o200k tokens, 18 outside system headers, so Bash truncation hides user code. Keep user regions.
  - Test: Run g++ -E on 5 files including Boost or Qt headers; drop flag-3 regions; check macro-expanded user code stays in order and tokens fall.
- **#730** [R] _small_ **ffmpeg, ffprobe and yt-dlp banner, progress and format-list pack** — ffmpeg prints a configuration line of --enable flags plus a libav version table, then frame= progress; yt-dlp -J lists every format and per-language caption URL. Drop the banner (or inject -hide_banner), fold progress, keep streams, duration, chapters. Synthetic ffmpeg banner: 499 tokens (o200k).
  - Test: Capture ffmpeg -i, ffprobe -show_streams and yt-dlp -J on 5 files; tokenise; assert codec, resolution, duration and errors survive; ask 5 stream questions.
- **#760** ★ [R] _medium_ **Collapse printed LlamaIndex NodeWithScore and LangChain Document reprs** — print(retriever.retrieve(q)) or response.source_nodes dumps each TextNode with constant fields: excluded_*_metadata_keys, relationships, metadata_template, text_template. Keep id, score, source, offsets, text. Synthetic reconstruction from memory: 8 nodes 2,916 vs 292 o200k tokens.
  - Test: Install llama-index, index five markdown files, print source_nodes from a top-8 query, run the collapse rule, compare o200k tokens and check text and scores survive.
- **#765** ★ [R] _medium_ **Strip layout geometry and confidences from document-parser JSON** — Unstructured, Docling and Azure Document Intelligence element dumps reportedly carry per-element coordinates or polygons, confidence scores and HTML duplicates of table text. Drop geometry and probabilities, keep text, type, page. Targets agents debugging ingestion by printing parsed elements.
  - Test: Parse a sample PDF with one of these parsers, dump elements as JSON, measure the token share of geometry keys, then confirm text, type and page survive the strip.
- **#783** [R] **Declarative per-command filter file with inline test fixtures** — rtk reportedly lets users add TOML filters (match command, strip or keep line regexes, head/tail, message on empty). A repo-level .ctxprune/filters.toml with embedded input and expected pairs lets agents add packs without Python; CI replays fixtures.
  - Test: Express three existing noise rules (pytest, npm install, cargo build) in the DSL; check byte-identical output versus the Python versions on 30 captured runs; time rule load per hook call.
- **#791** ★ [R] _medium_ **Drop API-defaulted fields from kubectl YAML and docker inspect** — Pod, Deployment and Service YAML carry server-filled defaults (dnsPolicy ClusterFirst, restartPolicy Always, terminationGracePeriodSeconds 30, protocol TCP); docker inspect repeats ~60 default HostConfig keys and MaskedPaths. A per-kind default table drops equal-valued fields; #33 drops only empty ones.
  - Test: Capture get pod, deploy, svc -o yaml from kind and 5 varied docker inspects; drop default-equal fields; verify non-default keys (Privileged, Binds, Memory, Ports) survive; tokenize before and after.
- **#795** [R] _small_ **Terraform state reads: drop private, sensitive_attributes, repeated provider addresses** — Reading terraform.tfstate or state pull shows per-instance 'private' (reportedly often base64 of null), empty sensitive_attributes, schema_version, dependencies, the full provider address on every resource, and tags_all copies of tags. #314 covers plan JSON only.
  - Test: Apply a 20-resource sample; cat tfstate; apply drop and hoist rules; check surviving attributes match terraform show; measure tokens; ask drift questions.
- **#800** ★ [R] _medium_ **gcloud logging read and aws logs envelope pack** — gcloud logging read YAML repeats insertId, logName, receiveTimestamp and the full resource.labels block per entry; aws logs filter-log-events adds eventId and ingestionTime. Hoist constant labels to a header, drop ids, keep time, severity, payload. Synthetic 5 entries: about 1005 vs 100 o200k.
  - Test: Run gcloud logging read --limit 50 against a test project or save a sample; diff kept fields against the original and count tokens.
- **#802** [R] _small_ **OTel Collector debug exporter: one line per record** — The collector debug/logging exporter prints about 10 lines per record: ObservedTimestamp, Timestamp, SeverityNumber, empty 'Trace ID:' and 'Span ID:', 'Flags: 0'. Fold to 'time SEV body k=v', drop empty and derivable fields. Synthetic 5 records: 500 vs 100 o200k.
  - Test: Run otelcol with the debug exporter and verbosity detailed on a demo app; compare tokens and confirm trace ids are kept when non-empty.
- **#803** [L] _small_ **Drop Java frame file names derivable from the class** — In 'at com.acme.InvoiceService.charge(InvoiceService.java:212)' the file name repeats the class name. Rewrite to '(:212)' when the stem equals the outer class (strip $Inner), keep it otherwise. Synthetic 12 frames: 196 vs 148 o200k tokens.
  - Test: Apply to 20 real Java and Kotlin traces; count tokens, then run the inverse expansion and compare byte-for-byte on the kept cases.
- **#805** [R] _small_ **GC trace lines to a pause summary** — JVM -Xlog:gc, Go GODEBUG=gctrace=1 and V8 --trace-gc print one decorated line per collection with timestamp, uptime, cause and heap sizes. Summarise count, total and max pause, heap trend; keep Full GC, long pauses, OOM lines. Ceiling: 20 lines 1230 vs 37 o200k.
  - Test: Run a JVM with -Xlog:gc for 60 s; compare tokens, and verify an injected 500 ms Full GC survives the fold.
- **#806** [R] _small_ **Slim tcpdump and tshark lines: options, seq numbers, ACK runs** — tcpdump -n lines repeat both endpoints, Flags, seq ranges, win and TS val/ecr options on every packet. Rewrite to delta time, direction, flags, length; fold runs of pure ACKs per flow; keep SYN, FIN, RST and retransmits. Synthetic 10 packets: 741 vs 300 o200k.
  - Test: Capture 5 s of local curl traffic with tcpdump -nn; apply the rewrite and check handshakes and resets are intact.
- **#856** [R] _small_ **Non-GitHub CI log packs: Jenkins, GitLab, Azure, Buildkite** — Jenkins prints [Pipeline] structure lines per stage and block; Azure DevOps ##[section]/##[command] markers; GitLab section_start/end; Buildkite ~~~ and --- group lines. Drop structure lines and fold passing sections, as #311 does for Actions. Formats reported from memory, unmeasured.
  - Test: Export console logs from a Jenkins pipeline and a GitLab job; strip marker lines and passing sections; assert failing stage and error lines survive; compare tokens.
- **#861** [R] _small_ **Actions debug-rerun logs: drop ##[debug] lines outside failures** — gh run rerun --debug (or ACTIONS_STEP_DEBUG) makes logs carry ##[debug] Evaluating, Expanded and Result lines for every expression and step. Drop them except in the failing step, where inputs may matter. Extends #311, which assumes normal logs.
  - Test: Rerun a failed workflow with --debug, pull logs, count ##[debug] share of tokens, drop outside the failing step, check root cause survives.
- **#865** [R] _tiny_ **pre-commit, husky and lint-staged output pack** — pre-commit prints each hook as name, dot leader padded to terminal width, then Passed or Skipped; husky and lint-staged add spinner and task lines. Fold passing and skipped hooks to a count, drop dot leaders, keep Failed hooks, exit codes and auto-fix diffs.
  - Test: Run git commit in a repo with 10 pre-commit hooks, passing and failing; fold; assert failed hook ids and diffs survive; compare tokens.
- **#883** [R] _small_ **Prune EXPLAIN ANALYZE plan nodes by share of actual time** — Postgres and MySQL EXPLAIN ANALYZE trees print cost, rows, loops, Buffers and Output lines for every node. Keep nodes above about 2% of total actual time, big estimate-versus-actual row misses, and the Planning/Execution footer; fold the rest to 'N fast nodes'. Original saved.
  - Test: Run EXPLAIN (ANALYZE, BUFFERS) on a six-table join in local Postgres; prune; ask slowest node, any seq scan, worst misestimate on both forms; tokenise.
- **#885** ★ [R] _medium_ **dbt console pack: fold START/OK pairs, dot leaders, debug SQL** — dbt build prints 'N of M START ... [RUN]' then an OK line, with dot leaders and clock prefixes; --debug adds connection, BEGIN/COMMIT and SQL echoes. One line per node; keep failures and warnings. Synthetic 10 models (from memory): 600 vs 170 o200k tokens.
  - Test: Run dbt build on jaffle-shop with DuckDB, plain and --debug; classify lines by template, fold, ask which node failed and how many tests passed; tokenise both.
- **#887** [R] _small_ **Fold identical idle GPU blocks in nvidia-smi output** — nvidia-smi prints a bordered block per GPU, so an 8-GPU idle node repeats equal fields. Fold equal-state GPUs into 'GPU 0-7 A100-80GB idle 4MiB 0% 34C'; keep busy GPUs and process rows verbatim. Synthetic 8 idle GPUs (layout approximate): 712 vs 43 o200k tokens.
  - Test: Capture nvidia-smi from a multi-GPU box or posted samples; fold, ask which GPUs are free and which processes hold memory, compare tokens; check XID or ECC errors survive.
- **#890** [R] _small_ **conda and mamba pack: solver chatter, Package Plan, build strings** — conda install/create prints metadata and solve chatter, a Package Plan, download tables and per-package build strings; conda list repeats a Channel column and build hashes. Keep name and version changes, conflicts and errors; steer conda env export to --no-builds (from memory).
  - Test: Run conda or mamba create with 15 packages, then conda list and env export; classify lines by template, filter, ask which packages changed and why a conflict failed; tokenise.
- **#894** [R] _small_ **Group Graphviz DOT and edge-list graphs by source node** — terraform graph, madge --dot, go mod graph and bazel query print one quoted edge per line, repeating '[root] ' prefixes and '(expand)' suffixes. Emit 'node -> a, b, c' adjacency lines, drop quotes. Synthetic 75 edges: 1,904 vs 459 o200k tokens.
  - Test: Capture terraform graph and go mod graph on real repos; convert; assert the edge set round-trips apart from dropped decorations; tokenise; ask reverse-dependency questions on both.
- **#974** [R] _small_ **strace: fold ENOENT probe storms by basename** — Imports, ld.so, locale and PATH lookups fail on one basename across many directories. Group failed openat/stat/access calls into 'name ENOENT x N dirs' and hoist the constant -f pid prefix. Measured: 53 of 280 lines held 2241 of 10718 o200k tokens. #508 keeps failures verbatim.
  - Test: Run strace -f -e trace=openat,stat,access python3 -c 'import json,argparse'; group ENOENT by basename; compare o200k tokens; check no non-ENOENT failure is folded.
- **#975** [R] _small_ **objdump and gdb disassembly: drop raw instruction-byte column** — objdump -d prints address, spaced hex encoding bytes, then mnemonic; agents read mnemonics. Dropping the byte column (keeping addresses for jump targets) cut 400 lines of /usr/bin/ls from 8842 to 5493 o200k tokens (-38%). Also fold nop/int3 padding runs.
  - Test: objdump -d a binary, strip the byte column, count o200k tokens; check every branch target still matches a kept address; ask a reader which function calls a given symbol.
- **#977** [R] _small_ **journalctl -x: strip catalog explanation blocks and failure hints** — From memory: journalctl -x/-xe appends ░░-prefixed catalog text (Subject, Defined-By, Support URL, 'unit has entered the failed state') per entry, and systemctl failures print 'See systemctl status / journalctl -xeu' hints. Text is constant per message ID; keep Result and exit-code lines.
  - Test: Capture journalctl -xeu on a deliberately failing unit; measure the ░░ line share in o200k tokens; verify Result and exit status lines survive the strip.
- **#979** ★ [R] _medium_ **nmap NSE blocks: vulners rows, cipher grades, cert fields** — vulners lists CVE rows with score plus a URL derivable from the ID; ssl-enum-ciphers lists each cipher per TLS version with a grade; ssl-cert dumps fields and hashes. Drop derivable URLs, fold A-grade ciphers to counts, keep weak ciphers and high scores. See #512.
  - Test: Run nmap -sV --script vulners,ssl-enum-ciphers,ssl-cert against a local test server or scanme.nmap.org; measure NSE token share; check weak ciphers and top CVEs survive.
- **#980** ★ [R] _medium_ **iptables-save and nft: fold generated chain families** — From memory: Docker, kube-proxy, Calico and CNI plugins create hundreds of chains like KUBE-SEP-<hash> and KUBE-SVC-<hash> differing only in hash and comment, plus [pkts:bytes] counters. Fold generated families to first example plus count, drop zero counters, keep hand-written chains and rules the command filtered for.
  - Test: Run iptables-save on a Docker or kind host; classify chains by prefix; compare o200k tokens before and after folding; check user chains and DROP rules survive verbatim.
- **#981** [R] _small_ **wget non-tty dot-progress lines: collapse or inject -nv** — Without a tty wget prints one dotted progress line per 50 KB, newline-separated, so CR-collapse (#10) misses it. Synthetic 400 lines: 10819 vs 89 o200k tokens. Collapse to first line plus the saved line, or add -nv in PreToolUse when no quiet flag is present.
  - Test: Download a 20 MB file with wget piped through cat; count progress-line tokens; confirm the final 'saved' line and any error or redirect lines survive.
- **#983** [R] _small_ **systemctl show, list-unit-files, analyze security: drop defaults** — From memory: systemctl show prints 100+ properties, many empty or default (infinity, no, [not set]); list-unit-files lists every enabled/static/masked file; systemd-analyze security prints a row per check. Keep non-default properties, group unit files by state with counts, keep failing checks and score. Complements #511.
  - Test: Run systemctl show, list-unit-files and systemd-analyze security on a systemd host; count empty/default lines and passing rows in o200k tokens; check non-default values survive.
- **#984** ★ [R] _medium_ **df, mount, findmnt: fold pod-UUID and container-ID path families** — Kubernetes and containerd hosts list hundreds of tmpfs kubelet pod mounts and overlay rootfs rows differing only in UUID or 64-hex ID. Synthetic 181 df lines: 12902 vs 85 o200k tokens. Fold by path template; keep real devices, near-full filesystems and rows the command named.
  - Test: Capture df and mount on a kind or k3s node; template-fold UUID and hex path segments; compare o200k tokens; check a full filesystem row is never folded.
- **#1009** [R] _tiny_ `MCP` **Compact MCP input-validation errors from pydantic, zod, JSON-RPC** — Failed calls return issue arrays; pydantic reportedly adds input_value, input_type and an errors.pydantic.dev URL per issue (from memory). Rewrite to one 'path: message' line per issue. Needs PostToolUseFailure or a wrapper, since is_error results may bypass the hook.
  - Test: Trigger bad calls on a FastMCP and a zod server, capture error text, tokenise, apply the rewrite, count retries needing dropped detail.
- **#1013** [M] _small_ **Anti-unify classifier-dropped lines into wildcard patterns, with negatives** — #261 keeps exact digit-masked templates, so unseen variants miss. Take lines the model drops at least 95% of the time, compute least general generalisations (anti-unification, Plotkin 1970), and reject any pattern matching a line the model kept or a needle.
  - Test: From 150 saved pytest, npm and cargo outputs build exact-template and anti-unified tables; on 50 held-out compare lines covered, false matches on needles, and chars removed.
- **#1022** [M] _small_ **Nearest-family transfer of learned filters to unseen commands** — Learned tables (#261) and synthesised filters (#646) need data per command. For an unseen CLI, fingerprint its first output by digit-masked line-shape histogram, find the closest known family by cosine, and apply that family's table with a stricter never-drop floor.
  - Test: Hold out 5 of 20 families; for each pick the nearest family and apply its table; report chars removed and needle recall versus no table and versus the model.
- **#1023** [R] _small_ **Mine agents' own pipe-suffix filters into per-family default filters** — In transcripts, collect post-filters agents append to commands (| tail -40, | grep -v node_modules, | grep -A5 FAIL). Per command family, recurring suffixes are the agent's own relevance judgement; promote them to default filters when the bare command runs large.
  - Test: Scan 100 transcripts, group by normalised base command, count suffixes; for the top 10 families check how often bare runs exceed 6,000 chars and which suffixes recur.
- **#1065** [R] _small_ **Drop docs-URL hint lines that restate an error code** — pydantic appends 'For further information visit https://errors.pydantic.dev/2.x/v/<type>' after every error, duplicating [type=...]: 83 of 213 o200k tokens in one 4-error sample. Similar lines, from memory: SQLAlchemy 'Background on this error', pytest '-- Docs:', Next 'Learn more'.
  - Test: Collect FastAPI, SQLAlchemy, pytest and Next error outputs; drop URL lines whose slug matches an in-line code; tokenise; confirm agents still resolve the errors.
- **#1066** [R] _small_ **Unbox Rich and Typer tracebacks, rejoin soft-wrapped paths** — Rich panels pad lines to 80 columns inside box borders and wrap paths mid-word; show_locals adds per-frame tables. One sample: 764 o200k tokens versus 221 for the stock traceback. Unbox, rejoin paths, keep locals for the last frame only.
  - Test: Crash a 5-frame Typer app with rich and show_locals on; unbox and rejoin; assert every path and line number round-trips; tokenise before and after.
- **#1068** ★ [R] _medium_ **Native-build failure digest for node-gyp, setuptools and extconf** — Failed native installs (sharp, psycopg2, nokogiri) dump gyp info/ERR blocks, gcc lines with identical flags, and extconf option lists. Keep the last compiler or 'checking for' error, tool versions once, hoisted flags. Sibling of #701 for other toolchains.
  - Test: Install sharp, psycopg2 and nokogiri with a missing system library; compare raw and digested tokens; check the missing library or header name survives.
- **#1071** [R] _small_ **Dedupe pytest records printed in both stderr and log sections** — With a StreamHandler on stderr, a failing test prints each record under 'Captured stderr call' and again, with level and logger prefix, under 'Captured log call' (reproduced with 2 records). Drop the plain copy when each line is a substring of a log-section message.
  - Test: Run failing tests from Django and Flask apps with stderr logging; count stderr lines matched in the log section; verify no unmatched line is dropped.
- **#1085** ★ [R] _medium_ **LaTeX build log pack for pdflatex and latexmk output** — pdflatex prints every opened file in nested parentheses, font and package info lines, and Overfull/Underfull hbox warnings. Keep '!' errors with their l.NN source line, undefined citations and references, a hbox count and the page count. Token share untested.
  - Test: Build 10 public LaTeX repos with latexmk plus one broken edit each; measure log tokens; ask for the error line and missing citation; assert no '!' line is ever dropped.
- **#1139** [R] _tiny_ `MCP` **Fold content-free issue comments: +1, bump, thanks, LGTM, emoji** — In GitHub MCP issue and PR comment lists, replace comments whose whole body is +1, bump, thanks, LGTM, 'any update?' or only emoji with a per-author count and a date range. Author and timestamp JSON per comment is the real cost. Original saved.
  - Test: Fetch comments for 20 busy public issues via gh api; count tokens in bodies matching the regex; spot-check 50 matches for a hidden maintainer decision.
- **#1148** [R] _small_ **CMake cache listings: drop help comments and blank lines** — cmake -LAH and CMakeCache.txt put a '//' help sentence and a blank line before every variable. One-file project: 1321 o200k tokens, 678 without them. Keep NAME:TYPE=value; help stays in the saved original.
  - Test: Run cmake -LAH on three real repos; count o200k tokens before and after; ask a reader which option controls a feature; compare accuracy.
- **#1149** [R] _small_ **Drop empty and default rows from toolchain config dumps** — go env, xcodebuild -showBuildSettings, mvn help:effective-pom and gradle properties print hundreds of default-valued entries (go env here: 389 tokens, 319 after dropping empty rows). Use per-tool default tables or an empty-project baseline; keep overrides and keys the command named.
  - Test: Capture each dump on a real project; diff against an empty-project baseline; measure tokens saved; check a reader still finds the JDK, SDK or proxy setting.
- **#1150** [R] _small_ **Linker undefined-reference floods: group by symbol, drop offsets** — GNU ld prints an 'in function' line plus 'src.cpp:(.text+0x2e): undefined reference to X' per call site; MSVC LNK2019 repeats the decorated name after the readable one (synthetic 52 vs 33 tokens). Group by symbol with caller list; drop offsets and decorated twins.
  - Test: Link objects missing 20 symbols with GNU ld, lld and MSVC; compare tokens; check a reader can still name the missing symbol and library.
- **#1156** [R] _small_ **Benchmark harness logs: fold warmup and iteration lines** — JMH, BenchmarkDotNet, criterion and go test -bench -count reportedly print per-iteration and warmup lines before a summary table (from memory). Keep the header once, the table and flagged outliers; fold iterations into min, mean, max per case.
  - Test: Run JMH or criterion on a five-case suite; compare tokens of full log versus table; ask a reader which case regressed.
- **#1160** [R] _small_ **SwiftPM and CocoaPods resolution: one line per package** — swift package resolve reportedly prints Fetching, Fetched, Computing, Computed and Working copy lines per dependency, repeating the URL (from memory; no swift installed here). pod install prints per-pod lines. Collapse to name, version, URL; keep errors verbatim.
  - Test: Run swift package resolve on a 20-dependency manifest on macOS CI; count tokens before and after; confirm failures are untouched.
- **#1164** ★ [R] _medium_ **macOS log show and log stream: drop activity columns** — Default style prints thread hex, type, activity ID, PID, TTL and microsecond dates with offset per line. Render time, process, subsystem:category and message, with date and PID hoisted. Synthetic 20 lines: 1088 vs 459 o200k tokens.
  - Test: Run log show --last 5m on a Mac; tokenise by column; compare against --style compact; confirm error-type lines keep subsystem and PID.
- **#1165** [L] _small_ **Python traceback frames to path:line function form** — Rewrite 'File "p.py", line 212, in charge' as 'p.py:212 charge', dropping boilerplate words per frame; path text unchanged. Synthetic 3-frame trace: 100 vs 85 tokens. Differs from #56 and #118, which drop frames or source lines.
  - Test: Collect 50 real tracebacks from pytest and scripts; rewrite with a regex; count tokens; ask the agent for failing file, line and function.
- **#1170** ★ [R] _medium_ **Windows Event XML to one flat key=value line** — Get-WinEvent ToXml and wevtutil /f:xml wrap events in Provider GUID, Correlation and Execution boilerplate plus <Data Name=...> tags. Emit time, channel, EventID, computer, then Name=value for non-dash data. Synthetic 4625 logon failure: 470 vs 73 tokens.
  - Test: Export 30 Security events as XML; flatten; check EventData names and values survive via a parser diff; ask the failing username and source IP.
- **#1171** [R] _small_ **gdb bt full: drop optimized-out and deep-frame locals** — bt full prints every local per frame; '<optimized out>' lines and locals of frames below the top few rarely help. Keep locals for the top 3 frames and app-code frames. Synthetic one frame: 92 vs 25 tokens. #426 only strips addresses.
  - Test: Crash a debug C program and open its core; run bt full; filter; check the faulting variable stays; count tokens per frame.
- **#1213** [R] _tiny_ **Drop runner-generated boilerplate annotations from check runs** — Each job gets generic annotations such as 'Process completed with exit code 1.' and, reportedly, Node.js-deprecation or set-output warnings, repeated across legs and runs. Drop a known-text list from check-run annotations and get_check_run output; keep annotations that carry file and line.
  - Test: Fetch annotations for 20 failed runs via gh api; count the share matching the list; check no annotation with a path or line is dropped.
- **#1214** [R] _small_ **Strip echoed step script and env block from failing step** — After failed-only fetches (#477) or passing-step folding (#311), the remaining failing step still opens with the echoed Run script, env: block and shell: line, often duplicating the workflow YAML. Replace with step name and a pointer; original stays saved.
  - Test: Take 10 failed-step logs; measure tokens in the Run header and env echo; strip; ask for root cause versus raw; check no env value needed for diagnosis is lost.
- **#1215** [R] _small_ **Fold empty-body COMMENTED reviews in get_reviews output** — Each single inline comment on a PR creates a review with state COMMENTED and empty body, so review lists carry many metadata-only entries with full user objects. Fold to a count per author; keep reviews with a body, APPROVED or CHANGES_REQUESTED.
  - Test: Fetch reviews for 15 PRs with inline comments via pull_request_read; count empty COMMENTED entries and their tokens; confirm the review-comments call still returns the comment text.
- **#1216** [R] _small_ **Fold release asset arrays to names and sizes** — list_releases and release JSON carry assets[] per release: one object per binary, checksum and signature, each with an uploader user object and URLs. Fold to name and size with a hoisted shared prefix; drop download counts and uploader. Original saved.
  - Test: Capture list_releases for five projects with many assets; tokenise the assets share; fold; ask which asset fits linux-arm64 and its size, versus raw.
- **#1225** [R] _small_ **Package inventory dumps: direct dependencies plus a transitive count** — pip list, npm ls --all, cargo tree, go list -m all, gem list and brew list print hundreds of rows (300 synthetic pip-list rows: 3.3k o200k tokens). Keep manifest-direct packages and ones named in the command or prompt; fold the rest to a count.
  - Test: Capture in two real repos; ask a reader for versions of direct and transitive packages, folded versus raw.
- **#1226** [R] _tiny_ **Drop man-page and --help footers: AUTHORS, COPYRIGHT, SEE ALSO** — Section triage for man output keeps NAME, SYNOPSIS, OPTIONS, EXAMPLES and drops AUTHOR, COPYRIGHT, SEE ALSO, REPORTING BUGS. Same for --help footers such as coreutils 'online help' and 'Full documentation' lines. tldr-pages is an alternative source of examples.
  - Test: Render 20 common man pages with col -bx; measure o200k tokens in dropped sections; confirm no flag description sits under those headings.
- **#1233** [R] _small_ **Security-group, NACL and firewall rules to one-liners** — aws ec2 describe-security-groups prints each rule as an object with empty Ipv6Ranges, PrefixListIds and UserIdGroupPairs arrays. Rewrite to 'tcp 443 10.0.0.0/8' lines; same for NACLs, route tables, gcloud firewall-rules. Synthetic 3-rule group: 313 vs 67 o200k tokens.
  - Test: Capture describe-security-groups, describe-network-acls and gcloud firewall-rules list --format=json from a real account; check every rule's protocol, ports, CIDR and source group survive the digest.
- **#1235** ★ [R] _medium_ **Digest cloud CLI debug traces: kubectl -v, aws --debug, TF_LOG** — Agents add kubectl -v=6/8, aws --debug, az --debug or TF_LOG=debug when stuck. Keep each request line with status and latency, the final error and auth or retry events; fold discovery GET storms and header or body dumps to counts. Original saved.
  - Test: Run kubectl get pods -v=8 and aws s3 ls --debug against kind and localstack; compare tokens and confirm an injected failure's status and message survive.
- **#1236** [R] _small_ **IaC synth output: strip CDK path metadata and asset hashes** — cdk synth and cdk diff print CloudFormation with Metadata aws:cdk:path per resource, a CDKMetadata resource, 64-hex asset S3Keys and 8-hex logical-ID suffixes. Drop metadata, alias hashes with a legend; try the same for sam, serverless package, pulumi preview.
  - Test: Synthesize a small CDK app with Lambda, S3 and IAM; measure tokens by field class and whether a reader can still map diff entries to construct paths.
- **#1238** ★ [R] _medium_ **Ansible setup facts: keep a curated subset** — ansible -m setup and gather_facts debug output dump hundreds of ansible_facts keys (env, mounts, devices, ssh host keys, python, interfaces). Keep distribution, kernel, CPU, memory, default IPs and keys named in the command; fold the rest to key names and sizes. Original saved.
  - Test: Run ansible -m setup on a container host and a VM; measure tokens by top-level key; check 10 questions about OS, memory and IP remain answerable.
- **#1240** [R] _small_ **kubectl diff and helm diff: fold generation and last-applied churn** — Diffs against live objects show a bumped metadata.generation and a rewritten last-applied-configuration annotation beside the real change. Fold only these known non-semantic hunks to one line; keep spec hunks and every destroy or replace line, respecting #1120.
  - Test: Change one Deployment image on a kind cluster; run kubectl diff; count tokens in churn lines versus the spec hunk, then verify the spec hunk is untouched.
- **#1242** [R] _small_ **Kind-aware healthy-row fold for any kubectl get table** — #793 folds pods only. Parse column tables for deployments (READY a/a), PVC Bound, jobs Complete, certificates Ready True, nodes Ready, plus argocd and flux rows Synced/Healthy; fold all-healthy rows to counts with name stems and keep any abnormal row verbatim.
  - Test: Capture get all -A, get pvc,job,node -A and argocd app list on a populated cluster; check no row with a non-healthy status column is folded.
- **#1243** [R] _small_ **Suppress stock cluster objects in kubectl listings** — Fresh clusters ship dozens of stock objects: system:* ClusterRoles and bindings, kube-root-ca.crt ConfigMaps per namespace, default ServiceAccounts, kube-public. Fold names matching a shipped stock list to 'N stock objects' and keep anything else; EKS, GKE and kind need separate lists.
  - Test: Run kubectl get clusterrole,clusterrolebinding,cm,sa -A on kind, EKS and GKE clusters; count rows matched by the stock list and check custom near-name objects survive.
- **#1252** [R] **Digest cargo metadata and go list -json output** — cargo metadata prints per-dependency source/req/kind/features and per-target src_path/edition/doctest flags; go list -json prints Deps, Imports and file lists per package. Emit name, version, path, workspace deps and targets. Per-tool pruning like #120.
  - Test: Run both on a mid-size workspace, count tokens raw versus digest, check the agent still answers 'which crates depend on X' and 'where is target Y's entry file'.
- **#1301** [R] _small_ **Windows PowerShell pack: CLIXML progress blobs, table padding, _x000D_ escapes** — Windows-hosted agents (Codex, Cursor, Cline) run PowerShell, where redirected stderr can carry '#< CLIXML' progress records and _x000D__x000A_ escaped errors, and Format-Table pads columns. Drop progress objects, decode escapes, trim padding. No current pack targets PowerShell.
  - Test: On a windows-latest CI runner run powershell -NoProfile -NonInteractive -Command with Install-Module, Get-ChildItem and a failing cmdlet; capture 2>&1 and count o200k tokens per artifact class.
- **#1313** [R] _small_ **Noise pack for dnf, yum, apk, composer, bundler, nuget, flutter** — Drop repo-metadata refresh lines, per-package download and verify rows and progress counters; keep errors, version conflicts and the final transaction summary. Existing packs cover npm, pip, cargo, apt, brew and conda, not these.
  - Test: Capture installs for each manager in containers, apply the pack, check reader QA on versions and failures, and count Claude tokens before and after.
- **#1325** ★ [R] _medium_ **IPython, nbconvert and PySpark failures: keep user frames, fold runtime** — IPython tracebacks add ANSI codes, multi-line and Cython frames; nbconvert echoes failing cell source first. PySpark errors repeat the message, then constant Driver stacktrace and py4j reflection frames. Keep failing cell and line, user frames, deepest cause. Hand-made IPython sample: 778 vs 46 tokens (o200k).
  - Test: Run nbconvert --execute on a notebook with a pandas KeyError, and a local PySpark job whose UDF raises ValueError; compare o200k tokens; confirm failing line, exception and UDF source survive.
- **#1327** ★ [R] _medium_ **sklearn search verbose: fold CV START/END pairs per candidate** — GridSearchCV(verbose=3) prints '[CV 2/5; 7/24] START' and END lines per fold and candidate, plus joblib '[Parallel]' progress; cv_results_ repeats params beside param_* columns. Fold to per-candidate mean scores; keep failures. Hand-made 24x5 grid, upper bound: 9,599 vs 491 tokens (o200k).
  - Test: Run GridSearchCV(SVC, verbose=3) on a toy dataset with 24 candidates and 5 folds; compare o200k tokens with a per-candidate fold; confirm best params survive.
- **#1328** ★ [R] _medium_ **Fixed-line packs for W&B, Comet, ClearML and Airflow logs** — W&B, Comet, Neptune and ClearML print login banners, run URLs, sparkline history and 'Synced N files' footers; Airflow task logs open with fixed dependency, runner and AIRFLOW_CTX_* lines. Fold to one header; keep run URL, metrics, task output and failures. Samples: 293 and 748 tokens.
  - Test: Run a toy wandb offline script and a local airflow tasks test; strip via fixed-line tables; count tokens removed; confirm final metrics and a failing task's traceback remain.
- **#1330** ★ [R] _medium_ **DB settings dumps: keep only non-default rows (pg_settings, SHOW VARIABLES)** — SELECT * FROM pg_settings lists hundreds of rows with description, unit and category columns; boot_val and source let a filter keep only rows differing from boot_val or with non-default source. MySQL SHOW VARIABLES and redis CONFIG GET * need a defaults table. Extends #1149.
  - Test: On a local Postgres run SELECT * FROM pg_settings; count rows with source='default'; compare o200k tokens before and after the filter; check every tuned parameter is kept.
- **#1336** [L] _small_ **Strip leading ./ from find, grep -r and tar listings** — find ., grep -rn pat . and tar -tf prefix every path with ./, which names the same file without it. Dropping it keeps later commands valid. Measured on 59 repo paths, o200k proxy: 435 to 375 tokens.
  - Test: Strip leading ./ in 50 saved find, rg and grep outputs; compare count_tokens; replay later Read and cat calls to confirm stripped paths resolve.
- **#1372** [R] _small_ **Mine log templates and levels from tool source code** — Parse logger and printf format strings from an installed CLI's package source to get exact line templates with severity, no sample outputs needed. Debug and info templates become drop candidates, error ones never-drop. A quick scan of pip found about 470 logger calls with levels.
  - Test: For pip, black and one npm CLI, extract templates, match 30 captured runs; report line coverage, level accuracy against observed severity, and chars removable without touching error templates.
- **#1374** [M] _small_ **Synthesised per-family keep-shield predicates, lower rate for remainder** — Instead of full drop filters (#646), a coding model writes a small must-keep predicate per command family (failure lines, summaries, counts) from about 10 captures. Shielded lines are exempt; the classifier then runs at a lower rate on the rest. Unlike generic shield (#91).
  - Test: For pytest, cargo and npm test, synthesise from 10 captures; on 10 held-out measure shield needle recall, then QA and tokens for shield plus rate 0.3 versus plain 0.5.
- **#1400** [R] _small_ `MCP` **Param and field pack for Tavily, Exa, Firecrawl, Brave MCPs** — Web search and scrape MCP servers reportedly return per-hit raw page text, images, favicons, scores and dates by default. PreToolUse sets cheap params (no raw content, small result count, main-content-only, highlights not full text); PostToolUse drops image, favicon and score fields.
  - Test: Verify param names in each server's tools/list. Run 10 queries per server with defaults versus trimmed; compare response tokens and whether the answer-bearing sentence survives; count follow-up fetches.

## Cross-call dedup (stateful)

- **#60** [L] **Re-read of unchanged file** — Replace with 'unchanged since read at turn N'; key on mtime and hash.
- **#61** [L] **Re-run with identical output** — Replace with a stub.
- **#62** [L] **Re-run with changed output** — Show only the delta vs the previous run plus 'N lines unchanged'; should help most in edit-and-test loops.
- **#63** [L] **Overlapping reads and greps** — Trim lines already shown earlier.
- **#64** [X] `eval` **Stub-safety test** — Invalidate state on PreCompact, /clear and subagent boundaries (subagents do not share context); measure the dangling-stub rate.
- **#123** [R] _small_ **Keep only the latest watch-mode compile cycle** — tsc --watch, vite, webpack --watch and vitest re-print a full report per change; earlier cycles' errors are stale. When a background-output poll returns several cycles, keep the last plus a cycle count. Needs BashOutput in the hook matcher.
  - Test: Verify that BashOutput results reach PostToolUse; run tsc --watch, edit a file three times, poll; compare full log with last-cycle-only and check stale errors never mislead.
- **#138** [L] _small_ **Elide output lines echoing the model's own Edit text** — Index lines from Edit new_string and Write content, which are guaranteed in context. Later pytest tracebacks, compiler snippets and git diff plus-lines matching verbatim become a pointer like 'your Edit #3, line 4'.
  - Test: Replay session JSONLs: count chars of tool_result lines of 30+ chars that exactly match earlier Edit/Write input lines; report share per tool.
- **#139** [L] _small_ `MCP` **Hunk-hash dedup across the git change lifecycle** — The same diff hunk reappears in git diff, git diff --cached, git show, gh pr diff and MCP PR diffs. Hash hunks per session; repeats become 'hunk unchanged: file @@ header'. Needs the verbatim skip relaxed for repeats.
  - Test: In transcripts, hash every diff hunk across Bash and MCP outputs; report bytes whose hunk hash was already seen earlier in the same session.
- **#140** [L] _small_ `MCP` **Content-hash stubs across Bash cat, Read and MCP file tools** — Key repeat detection on the text hash, not path or command, so cat, sed -n, Read and MCP get_file_contents of the same bytes collapse to one earlier occurrence. Covers verbatim-skipped commands too, since the model already has the text.
  - Test: Hash every file-content tool_result across Read, Bash cat/sed -n/head and MCP file tools in transcripts; count byte-identical repeats that cross tool type.
- **#141** ★ [R] _medium_ **Line-shift-proof diagnostic delta for edit-compile loops** — Key linter, type-checker and compiler diagnostics by (file, rule, message) ignoring line and column, so edits that shift lines do not defeat delta. Show new and resolved items plus 'N unchanged'; originals stay on disk.
  - Test: Run mypy, tsc or ruff across 10 successive small edits; compare plain line-diff size against normalised-key delta size in tokens.
- **#142** ★ [R] _medium_ **Run-invariant baseline: collapse lines present in every prior run** — For a normalised command family, keep the intersection of lines across the last K runs (banners, versions, fixed warnings). Lines identical in all prior runs collapse to a count, originals saved. Learns per-repo noise without hand-written packs.
  - Test: Run npm test or pytest five times with small edits; count lines in all prior runs and check no failing-test line is hidden without a count.
- **#144** ★ [L] _medium_ **Own-edit-aware re-read: show only hunks the model changed** — Keep a shadow of the last Read per file and replay the model's Edit old/new pairs onto it. A later Read with no foreign changes is rewritten to the edited ranges plus three lines of context, with 'rest unchanged since turn N'.
  - Test: Replay transcripts: find Reads of files whose only changes since the previous Read are the model's own Edits; sum lines avoidable by hunk-only reads.
- **#148** [R] _small_ **Path-set delta across find, ls, git ls-files and rg --files** — Parse listings from different commands into path sets. A second listing that overlaps an earlier one by most entries prints only added and removed paths plus the covered count. Keys on entities, not command text.
  - Test: Extract path sets from consecutive listings in transcripts; compute Jaccard overlap with the best earlier listing; total paths savable at 80% overlap.
- **#149** [X] **Stub recency horizon: re-show originals past a token distance** — Stubs may fail when the original sits far back in a long context. Track tokens since the original; beyond a threshold, emit the full text again or a short excerpt. Distinct from invalidation at compaction boundaries.
  - Test: Build long contexts with a stubbed result at 5k, 20k, 60k and 120k tokens back; measure how often the model answers a question needing it correctly.
- **#186** ★ [M] _large_ `busts-cache` **Cache-aware proxy that stubs or compresses old tool results** — A local reverse proxy (ANTHROPIC_BASE_URL, OPENAI_BASE_URL) or an owned loop's condenser (OpenHands, Agent SDK) swaps tool_results older than N turns for stubs plus saved ids. Memoize each rewrite and batch evictions so earlier bytes never change and one cache rewrite pays off.
  - Test: Verify that the base-URL override works; replay a recorded 40-turn transcript at N=5/10/20; assert byte-identical prefixes, compare billed cache tokens and how often stubbed results get re-fetched.
- **#285** [M] _small_ `busts-cache` **Prune tool results in compaction requests when cache is cold** — The summarisation call sends the whole history to get a short summary. If an idle gap already expired the cache, the prefix is rewritten anyway, so run ctxprune over its tool_results. With a warm cache this likely loses, since reads cost 0.1x.
  - Test: Capture a /compact request via logging proxy; measure tool_result token share; compare pruned versus full cost cold and warm; have a reader answer questions from both summaries.
- **#288** ★ [R] _medium_ `busts-cache` **Stub old Write and heredoc tool_use inputs in request history** — A Write input carries the whole file and stays in history after the file is on disk; existing proxy ideas only touch tool_results. Proxy replaces aged Write content or Bash heredoc bodies with path, line count and hash; tool_use inputs are reportedly not signature-checked.
  - Test: Sum tokens of Write and heredoc inputs older than 10 turns across transcripts; replay stubbed history and count how often the model re-Reads or contradicts the earlier content.
- **#349** ★ [L] _large_ `MCP` **Delta-encode repeated browser accessibility snapshots per tab** — Playwright-style MCP servers and Chrome extensions return a full accessibility tree with [ref=eN] ids after most actions. Keep the last tree per tab; emit changed subtrees plus 'N lines unchanged', with refs stable. context-mode reportedly sandboxes snapshots instead.
  - Test: Replay a 20-step browser task logging every snapshot; first verify refs stay stable across snapshots; tokenize full versus delta; confirm every ref the next click used is still visible.
- **#390** [R] _small_ **Order-insensitive, volatile-masked fingerprints for re-run stubs and deltas** — Verified here: rg without --sort printed the same 117k lines in a different order on each of 8 runs (sorted hashes identical), so exact-hash re-run detection never fires. Hash the sorted line multiset after masking durations, PIDs and tmp names; diff as line sets.
  - Test: Run rg, pytest -n 4 and make -j repeatedly on one tree; count exact versus sorted-and-masked fingerprint matches, then check stubbed outputs lose no failing-test lines.
- **#405** [X] `eval` **Measure post-compaction re-read tax; inject a file manifest** — After auto-compact the model reportedly re-reads files it already saw; terse (qdf-hook) reportedly forces a full re-read plus a file manifest around compaction. Measure first, then test a path, line-count and hash manifest that lets reads use offsets.
  - Test: Find compaction boundaries in transcripts; sum Read and cat chars in the next 10 turns that repeat files read before the boundary; compare with the manifest's token cost.
- **#450** [M] _small_ `busts-cache` **Compress aged assistant narration in request history** — A proxy runs the classifier over assistant text blocks older than N turns (narration, plans, recaps), which are re-read every turn. Thinking blocks are signed and left alone. Memoize rewrites and batch them at cold gaps so cached bytes stay stable.
  - Test: Sum tokens of assistant text blocks older than 10 turns in long interactive transcripts; replay compressed history and ask 10 recall questions about earlier decisions versus uncompressed.
- **#530** [L] _small_ **Seed ledger with CLAUDE.md, @-mentions, prompts and assistant text** — Index text the model already holds, not only tool results and own Edits: CLAUDE.md and imports, @-mentioned file attachments, user prompts, assistant messages. A later Read, cat or pasted-trace echo of those bytes becomes 'already in context: source'.
  - Test: Mine transcripts for Reads or cats of files earlier attached via @-mention or quoted in a prompt; sum duplicated tokens per session.
- **#533** [R] _small_ **Cross-command error stubs keyed on exception signature** — The same traceback or compiler error reaches the model via different commands (python x.py, pytest, a node script). Fingerprint type, message and app frames with paths, addresses and line numbers masked; repeats become 'same error as turn 12'. #17 and #126 fold within one output.
  - Test: Mine transcripts for tracebacks sharing type plus message across distinct commands; count repeat tokens; check the model still fixes the error from the stub.
- **#582** [M] _small_ `busts-cache` **Graded age-based decay ladder in OpenHands condenser pipeline** — Replace mask-or-keep (ObservationMaskingCondenser) with chained condensers: full, pruned at 0.5 after k turns, 0.2 after 2k, then stub. Release in batches like AmortizedForgettingCondenser so cached prefixes stay stable. Keep rate falls with age, not only size.
  - Test: Replay one recorded OpenHands trajectory offline; integrate resident tokens times turns under mask-only versus the ladder, counting cache-rewrite events; then ask recall questions on aged observations.
- **#593** ★ [R] _medium_ `busts-cache` **Todo completion as deterministic fold boundary for old results** — ReFold (reportedly) folds turns the agent calls finished; TokenPilot (arXiv 2606.17016) tracks active, completed and evictable segments. A TodoWrite or TaskUpdate completed event marks a span whose tool results become one-line stubs with saved ids, via proxy or resume surgery.
  - Test: From transcripts, find completed-todo events; sum tool-result tokens in each span and the share never mentioned (path or identifier) afterwards. Replay stubbed copies and ask recall questions.
- **#594** [R] _small_ `busts-cache` **Recoverability-ranked eviction: stub re-obtainable results first** — Context Window Lifecycle (arXiv 2606.11213) evicts the oldest and most recoverable content by dependency graph. Classify results: unchanged-file Read, git show, ls and grep are cheap to regenerate; one-shot test runs, network fetches and deleted-file output are not. Stub the first class first.
  - Test: Label every tool result in 20 transcripts as regenerable or not; report token share per class and how often each class was re-read or re-run later in the session.
- **#595** [R] `MCP, busts-cache` **Agent-callable fold tool honored by a history-rewriting proxy** — SWE-Compressor ('Context as a Tool', ACL 2026 Findings) and AgentFold let the agent choose when to condense history. Expose a fold(ids, note) MCP tool using the short ids in hook notes; a proxy swaps those results for the note and saved ids.
  - Test: In an Agent SDK loop on 10 tasks, compare agent-chosen folds with fixed older-than-N stubbing: billed tokens, refetches, pass rate, and whether the agent uses the tool unprompted.
- **#688** [R] _small_ **Read delta for files changed outside the model's edits** — qdf-hook reportedly returns a unified diff against its cached copy when a file changed between reads. Own-edit hunks are covered by #144; changes from git pull, branch switch, formatters or teammates are not. Emit a diff only while the earlier Read is in context.
  - Test: From transcripts, find re-Reads where mtime changed but no Edit touched the path; sum changed lines versus file length; check how often an Edit follows.
- **#763** ★ [L] _medium_ **Chunk-id seen-set across retrieval calls in agentic RAG** — Paraphrased queries to a retrieval tool return overlapping chunks in new order with new scores, so whole-output hashes miss them. Key on chunk or node id; replace chunks already in context with 'chunk X, shown in call N'. Related to #145.
  - Test: Replay a 10-question session against a docs index; measure the share of returned chunk ids already seen in the last five calls and their token cost.
- **#773** [R] `busts-cache` **Retroactively prune aged results to lines the agent reused** — After N turns a proxy checks whether protected atoms or lines of a big tool result reappear in later assistant text or tool inputs. None: stub it. Some: keep reused lines plus two context lines. A saved copy stays. #169 only trains on this signal.
  - Test: On 20 sessions, take tool results over 2k chars; measure the token share of lines reused in the next 5 turns; replay with stubs or trimmed results; count refetches.
- **#775** [R] _tiny_ `busts-cache` **Regex-strip tool-announcing narration from aged assistant text** — Rule-based sibling of #450: in a history-rewriting proxy, delete sentences such as 'Let me read the file.' or 'Now I'll run the tests.' from assistant text blocks that sit directly before a tool_use and are older than N turns.
  - Test: Count tokens of assistant text blocks under 15 words directly before tool_use in 10 transcripts, aged over 10 turns; replay with them removed and ask 10 recall questions.
- **#824** [R] _small_ `busts-cache` **Stub dead old_string text of aged Edit calls** — A history-rewriting proxy replaces old_string in Edit/MultiEdit tool_use blocks older than N turns with '[N lines replaced]'. That text no longer exists on disk and an earlier Read already showed it; new_string stays. Complements #288, which covers Write and heredocs.
  - Test: Sum old_string tokens of Edit calls older than 10 turns; verify that rewritten tool_use history is accepted; replay stubbed history, ask 10 questions about previous values, count re-Reads.
- **#902** [X] `eval` **Compare stub back-reference formats the model can resolve** — Stubs like 'unchanged since turn 12' assume a turn index the model cannot see in its context. Compare pointer grammars: turn number, command text plus first-line quote, or a short tag the hook appends to long outputs. Pick the one resolved without re-running.
  - Test: Build a 30-turn transcript with one stub per format; ask what the earlier result said about X; score correct answers and count re-runs per format.
- **#905** [R] _small_ **Subtract the model's own edited files from git status** — A session set of Edit and Write targets is built from PostToolUse. git status, diff --stat and --name-only lines for paths the model itself edited become a count ('11 files = your edits'); only foreign, untracked or unexpected paths are listed.
  - Test: Scan transcripts: for each git status, diff --stat or --name-only result, count paths equal to earlier Edit/Write file_path values; report lines and tokens avoidable.
- **#908** [L] _small_ **Line-keyed delta for repeated git diff after further edits** — Hunk-hash dedup (#139) fails when a hunk grows by a few lines, the typical edit-diff-edit loop. Key each +/- line by (file, content); print only unseen lines under their current hunk header plus 'N lines as before', so patches stay readable.
  - Test: Make 8 incremental edits to one function, running git diff after each. Compare tokens of full diffs, hunk-hash dedup and line-keyed delta.
- **#909** [L] _small_ **Encode a Read as a unified diff against sibling file** — Config variants, locale catalogs, per-env Terraform, similar test files and vendored copies are read back to back. If the new file has difflib ratio of 0.7+ with a file already read, emit 'same as PATH with these changes' plus the diff. Exactly reversible.
  - Test: In three repos, list same-directory file pairs with ratio at least 0.7; tokenise the full second file versus the unified diff; count pairs transcripts read within ten turns.
- **#911** [R] _small_ **Drop line templates seen in three distinct commands' outputs** — Per-family baselines (#142) miss boilerplate shared across commands: npm deprecation warnings, runtime banners, repeated table headers, Python warnings. Mask digits and ids, count distinct commands per template in a session, and fold a template from its third distinct command on, with a count.
  - Test: Over transcripts, template-normalise output lines, count templates in 3+ distinct command strings, report their token share and check no failing-test or error lines are among them.
- **#912** [R] _small_ `MCP` **Learn per-host page chrome from the first fetch** — Raw curl and fetch-MCP pages from one docs site repeat nav, footer and cookie lines; fixed regexes age badly. After two fetches from one host, lines present in both are chrome: drop them from later pages, keeping the first page whole as the reference.
  - Test: Fetch five pages from each of three docs sites; compute line sets shared by two pages; count their tokens; verify by hand that no shared line is body content.
- **#970** [X] `eval` **Keep-the-failures A/B before stubbing dead-end attempts** — The Manus context-engineering post reportedly advises leaving failed actions and stack traces in context so the model updates its beliefs. Stub rules for reverted or failed spans (#338) should be tested against that claim by counting repeated identical failures.
  - Test: Replay 15 recorded failing-then-fixed tasks with failed results kept versus one-line stubs; compare repeated failing commands, turns to green and tokens.
- **#1055** [R] _small_ `busts-cache` **Stub inputs of errored tool calls after a few turns** — OpenCode's dynamic-context-pruning plugin reportedly has a purge-errors strategy: after N turns, inputs of failed calls (rejected Edit, failed Write, errored heredoc) become a stub and the error text stays. Differs from #824, which stubs old_string of successful Edits.
  - Test: Sum tool_use input tokens of calls whose result is an error and which are older than 5 turns, across transcripts; replay stubbed history and count repeated identical mistakes.
- **#1145** [R] _small_ **Fault-history pinning: stop re-stubbing re-requested paths** — Pichay (arXiv 2603.09023, reportedly) detects a page fault when a model re-requests evicted content and pins that page for the session. Apply to dedup stubs (#60, #140): after one re-read of a stubbed path or hash, never stub it again.
  - Test: Replay 20 transcripts through a stub simulator; count re-reads within 10 turns of a stub and the tokens spent; compute what pin-after-first-fault would have avoided.
- **#1257** [R] `busts-cache` **Fold aged results at git commit and ExitPlanMode boundaries** — A commit makes earlier diffs, Edit echoes and Reads of the committed files re-obtainable from git; plan approval makes exploration reads redundant with the approved plan. Treat each event as a fold boundary, stubbing those results at the cold-cache or resume point.
  - Test: Replay transcripts: sum tokens of tool results touching committed files before each commit, and exploration results before ExitPlanMode; check how often later turns refetch them.
- **#1258** [R] _small_ `busts-cache` **Red-to-green fold: stub failing-run logs once tests pass** — When a test or build command first goes green after a red streak, the middle failing logs are dead history. Stub all but the first and last red outputs, keeping the final error-signature line. Trigger is the status flip, not todo state.
  - Test: Replay transcripts for red-then-green runs of one command family; total middle-red tokens and check whether later turns quote anything from them.
